3/2

The signature is required to satisfy

\displaystyle s_{2}(\textit{þ}^{i}\textit{þ}^{\prime j}\{\textit{ð},\textit{ð}^{\prime}\}^{k}f)=s_{2}(f)+0\times i+1\times j+\tfrac{1}{2}\times k,

\displaystyle s_{2}(f_{1}\cdot f_{2})=s_{2}(f_{1})+s_{2}(f_{2}).

We define the following
scale-invariant norms
for each quantity
f
with its signature
s_{2}(f)
:

\displaystyle||f||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\equiv a^{-s_{2}(f)}|u|^{2s_{2}(f)+1}||f||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})},

\displaystyle||f||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\equiv a^{-s_{2}(f)}|u|^{2s_{2}(f)}||f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})},

\displaystyle||f||_{L^{1}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\equiv a^{-s_{2}(f)}|u|^{2s_{2}(f)-1}||f||_{L^{1}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}.

The above norms satisfy the Hölder inequalities

\displaystyle||f_{1}\cdot f_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\frac{1}{|u|}||f_{1}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}||f_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})},

(23)

\displaystyle||f_{1}\cdot f_{2}||_{L^{1}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\frac{1}{|u|}||f_{1}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}||f_{2}||_{L^{1}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})},

(24)

\displaystyle||f_{1}\cdot f_{2}||_{L^{1}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\frac{1}{|u|}||f_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}||f_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}.

(25)

Thus, it follows that making use of these norms that if all terms are
order
1
, then the nonlinear terms can be treated as lower order
terms if
|u|\gg 1
. Finally, we define the following scale-invariant norms along the lightcones:

\displaystyle||f||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}

\displaystyle\equiv\int_{0}^{v}||f||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime},

\displaystyle||f||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\equiv\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||f||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}.

3.2
Bootstrap norms

In this section we introduce the norms that will be used to set up our
main bootstrap argument. In the following, let
\hat{\Gamma}\equiv\{\rho,\tau,\pi,{\underline{\omega}},{\underline{\chi}}\}
,

\hat{\Psi}=\{{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1},\Psi_{2},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3},\Psi_{4}\}
.

(i) Norms at spheres of constant
u
,
v
.

For
0\leq i\leq 6
, we define

\displaystyle\Gamma_{i,\infty}(u,v)\equiv

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\sigma||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\hat{\Gamma}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\lambda||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\mu||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\tilde{\mu}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})},

\displaystyle\Psi_{i,\infty}(u,v)\equiv\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\hat{\Psi}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})},

\displaystyle\phi_{i,\infty}(u,v)\equiv\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\phi_{0}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\phi_{1}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})},

\displaystyle\zeta_{i,\infty}(u,v)\equiv

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\zeta_{0}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\{\zeta_{1},\zeta_{2},\zeta_{3},{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4},{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}\}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\{\zeta_{4},\zeta_{5}\}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}.

For
0\leq i\leq 10
, we define

\displaystyle\Gamma_{i,2}(u,v)\equiv

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\hat{\Gamma}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\tilde{\mu}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})},

\displaystyle\phi_{i,2}(u,v)\equiv\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\phi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\phi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})},

\displaystyle\zeta_{i,2}(u,v)\equiv\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\zeta_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\{\zeta_{1},\zeta_{2},\zeta_{3}\}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\{\zeta_{4},\zeta_{5}\}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}.

For
0\leq i\leq 9
, we define

\displaystyle\Psi_{i,2}(u,v)\equiv\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\hat{\Psi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})},

\displaystyle{\tilde{\zeta}}_{i,2}(u,v)\equiv||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\{{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4},{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}\}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}.

(ii) Norms at null hypersurfaces

For
0\leq i\leq 10
, we define

\displaystyle{\bm{\Psi}}_{i}(u,v)\equiv\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\{{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1},\Psi_{2},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}\}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))},

\displaystyle\underline{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{i}(u,v)\equiv\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\{\Psi_{2},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3},\Psi_{4}\}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}.

For
0\leq i\leq 11
, we define

\displaystyle{\bm{\phi}}_{i}(u,v)\equiv\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i}\phi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))},\quad\underline{{\bm{\phi}}}_{i}(u,v)\equiv\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i}\phi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))},

\displaystyle{\bm{\zeta}}_{i}(u,v)\equiv

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i}\zeta_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i}\{\zeta_{1},\zeta_{3}\}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}

\displaystyle+||\frac{1}{|u|}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\mathcal{D}^{i}\zeta_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))},

\displaystyle\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}_{i}(u,v)\equiv

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i}\{\zeta_{1},\zeta_{3}\}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i}\zeta_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle+||\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\mathcal{D}^{i}\zeta_{5}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}.

Here

\displaystyle||\frac{1}{|u|}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\mathcal{D}^{i}\zeta_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}\equiv\frac{1}{|u|}\left(\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle||\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\mathcal{D}^{i}\zeta_{5}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\equiv\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\zeta_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Moreover for
0\leq i\leq 10
, define

\displaystyle\tilde{{\bm{\zeta}}}_{i}(u,v)\equiv

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\mathcal{D}^{i}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))},\quad\tilde{\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}}_{i}(u,v)\equiv||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\mathcal{D}^{i}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}.

Remark 3
.

We denote the supremum over
u
and
v
of the above defined bootstrap norms.

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}}\equiv\sum_{i\leq 6}(\Gamma_{i,\infty}+\Psi_{i,\infty}+\phi_{i,\infty}+\zeta_{i,\infty})+\sum_{i\leq 10}(\Gamma_{i,2}+\phi_{i,2}+\zeta_{i,2})+\sum_{i\leq 9}(\Psi_{i,2}+\tilde{\zeta}_{i,2}),

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}\equiv\sum_{i\leq 10}(\Psi_{i}+\underline{\Psi}_{i}),\quad\bm{\phi}\equiv\sum_{i\leq 11}(\bm{\phi}_{i}+\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{i}),\quad\bm{\zeta}\equiv\sum_{i\leq 11}(\bm{\zeta}_{i}+\underline{\bm{\zeta}}_{i})+\sum_{i\leq 10}(\bm{{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{i}+\underline{\bm{{\tilde{\zeta}}}}_{i}),

and let
{\bm{\Gamma}}_{0}
,
\bm{\Psi}_{0}
,
\bm{\phi}_{0}
and
\bm{\zeta}_{0}
denote the norm on the initial light cones.
We also make use of the
initial data quantity

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{0}\equiv\sup_{0\leq v\leq 1}\mathcal{I}_{0}(v),

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{0}(v)\equiv\sum_{j=0}^{1}\sum_{i=0}^{15}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||\textit{þ}^{j}(|u_{\infty}|\mathcal{D})^{i}(\sigma,\phi_{0})||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}.

We also use boldface to denote the supremum over
u
and
v

of the norms of a specific quantity on the light cone for convenience,
for example we denote

\displaystyle\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]\equiv\sup_{u}\sum_{i=0}^{11}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i}\phi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))},\quad\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{5}]\equiv\sup_{v}\sum_{i=0}^{11}||\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\mathcal{D}^{i}\zeta_{5}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}.

We make use of non-boldface to denote the supremum over
u

and
v
of the norm of a specific quantity on 2-sphere,
for example we denote

\displaystyle\Gamma[\lambda]\equiv

\displaystyle\sup_{u,v}\left(\sum_{i=0}^{10}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\sum_{i=0}^{6}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\lambda||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\right),

\displaystyle\zeta[\zeta_{0}]\equiv

\displaystyle\sup_{u,v}\left(\sum_{i=0}^{10}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\zeta_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\sum_{i=0}^{6}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\zeta_{0}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\right).

In the rest of the article, for simplicity we use
A\lesssim B
to denote that there exist a
constant
C>0
which is independent of
a
, such that
A\leq CB
. We
do not distinguish between
\lesssim
and
\leq
when there is no
source of confusion.

3.3
Strategy of the bootstrap argument

We start with the bound

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}}_{0}+\bm{\Psi}_{0}+\bm{\phi}_{0}+\bm{\zeta}_{0}\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0},

and want to prove that

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}}(u,v)+\bm{\Psi}(u,v)+\bm{\phi}(u,v)+\bm{\zeta}(u,v)\lesssim(\mathcal{I}_{0})^{2}+\mathcal{I}_{0}+1

(26)

in the region

\displaystyle\mathbb{D}=\big\{(u,v)|u_{\infty}\leq u\leq-a/4,\ \ 0\leq v\leq 1\big\}.

Then, by a last-slice argument, a solution to the Einstein–Weyl system
exists in the domain
\mathscr{D}
, from this uniform bound.

To achieve this we make the following bootstrap assumptions

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}}+\bm{\Psi}+\bm{\phi}+\bm{\zeta}\leq\mathcal{O}

(27)

for large
\mathcal{O}
such that

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{0}\ll\mathcal{O},

but also

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}^{20}\leq a^{\frac{1}{16}}.

We need to show that the following set

\displaystyle\bm{I}=\{u|u_{\infty}\leq u\leq-a/4,\ \eqref{Hypothesis}\ holds\ for\ every\ 0\leq v\leq 1\}

is both open and closed.

Firstly, from the local existence results of CIVP for Einstein-Weyl spinor system; see
[
26
]
,
there exists a small
\varepsilon
such that for

u_{\infty}\leq u\leq u_{\infty}+\varepsilon
we have

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}}+\bm{\Psi}+\bm{\phi}+\bm{\zeta}\lesssim 2\mathcal{I}_{0}\ll\mathcal{O}.

That means
[u_{\infty}\leq u\leq u_{\infty}+\varepsilon]\subseteq\bm{I}
, i.e.
\bm{I}
is a closed set.
Secondly, in sections
4
,
5
and
6
we show

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}}(u,v)\lesssim

\displaystyle(\bm{\Psi}(u,v)+\bm{\phi}(u,v)+\bm{\zeta}(u,v))^{2}+\mathcal{I}_{0}+1,

\displaystyle\bm{\phi}(u,v)\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{0}+1,\quad\bm{\zeta}(u,v)\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}+1,\quad\bm{\Psi}(u,v)\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}+1.

These results improve the upper bounds in the bootstrap assumption (
27
).
Consequently, the continuity of solutions and local existence arguments lead to that

\bm{I}
can be extended a bit forward along
u
. This implies that
\bm{I}
is open.
Together with the closedness, one concludes that
\bm{I}\equiv[u_{\infty}\leq u\leq-a/4]

and the uniform bound in (
26
) holds.

3.4
Estimates for the components of the frame

We introduce the basic estimates on the components of the frame with the bootstrap assumption.
As the bootstrap assumptions are the same as in paper
[
8
,
15
]
,
the ensuing conclusions remain unchanged,
therefore we provide only a brief outline and the resulting statements.
For the component
Q
, apply the definition of
{\underline{\varrho}}
one has

\displaystyle|Q-1|\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|}.

Hence when
a
is sufficiently large,
||Q,Q^{-1}||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}
are close to
1
.
Following the same strategy we have the following estimate for the area of
\mathcal{S}_{u,v}
:

\displaystyle|\mbox{Area}(S_{u,v})-2\pi u^{2}|\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|}.

under the bootstrap assumption of
\rho
.

3.5
Important inequalities

We mainly use the Grönwall inequality and Sobolev embedding in the proof.
We list these inequalities in scale-invariant form below:

Proposition 1
.

\displaystyle||\phi||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim||\phi||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}||\textit{þ}\phi||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime},

(28a)

\displaystyle||\phi||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim||\phi||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{0,v})}+\int_{0}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\textit{þ}^{\prime}\phi||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}.

(28b)

Moreover, for the ingoing equation we have

Proposition 2
.

For transport equations of the form

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\Gamma=\lambda_{0}\mu\Gamma+F

one has that

\displaystyle a^{s_{2}(\Gamma)}|u|^{\lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(\Gamma)}||\Gamma||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim a^{s_{2}(\Gamma)}|u_{\infty}|^{\lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(\Gamma)}||\Gamma||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}a^{s_{2}(F)}|u^{\prime}|^{\lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(F)}||F||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime},

where
s_{2}(F)=s_{2}(\textit{þ}^{\prime}\Gamma)=s_{2}(\Gamma)+1
.

Making use of the estimate on the area
\mbox{Area}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})\sim|u|^{2}
,
we have the following Sobolev inequalities

Proposition 3
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions, in terms of scale-invariant norms one has that

\displaystyle||\phi||_{L_{sc}^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\sum_{i\leq 2}||\left(a^{1/2}\mathcal{D}\right)^{i}\phi||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}.

(29)

For the details of the proof we refer reader to
[
8
,
15
]
.

3.6
Commutators

In the following let
H_{0}\equiv\textit{þ}f
and

H_{k}\equiv\textit{þ}\textit{ð}^{k}f
, we have that

\displaystyle H_{k}=\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\pi,\tau)^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}H_{0}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma)\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}f.

Here we write
\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}
to
denote
\textit{ð}^{j_{1}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{j_{2}}\Gamma...\textit{ð}^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma

where
i_{1}\geq 0
,
i_{2}\geq 1
,
j_{1}
,
j_{2}
, …,

j_{i_{2}}\in\mathbb{N}
and
j_{1}+j_{2}+...+j_{i_{2}}=i_{1}
.
Similarly, letting
G_{0}\equiv\textit{þ}^{\prime}f
and

G_{k}\equiv\textit{þ}^{\prime}\textit{ð}^{k}f
, we have that

\displaystyle G_{k}=-k\mu\textit{ð}^{k}f+\textit{ð}^{k}G_{0}+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\textit{ð}^{i}\mu\textit{ð}^{k-i}f+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\textit{ð}^{i}\lambda\textit{ð}^{k-i}f.

The above expressions for
H_{k}
and
G_{k}
neither show the exact
constants in each sum nor the T-weight of
f
. The expressions of
H_{k}
and
G_{k}
do
not distinguish the
ð
and
\textit{ð}^{\prime}
and their complex
conjugates. The reason behind this can be traced back to the definition
of the norms. Further discussion can be found in
[
15
]
.

4
L^{2}(\mathcal{S})
estimates

In this section, under the
bootstrap assumptions

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}}+\bm{\Psi}+\bm{\phi}+\bm{\zeta}\leq\mathcal{O},

we estimate the
L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S})
norm of next-to-leading derivative of each quantity.
We show that

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}}\lesssim(\bm{\Psi}+\bm{\phi}+\bm{\zeta})^{2}+\mathcal{I}_{0}+1.

The strategy is to make use of the transport equation and the Grönwall inequalities.
Technically, due to the property of Hölder inequality in the scale-invariant norm (
23
),
products of nonlinear terms are small,
Take
\Gamma\phi_{j}
as an example, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{j}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{1}}\Gamma||...||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{4}}\bm{\phi}||

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{4}}\bm{\phi}||\leq\frac{1}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-i}\bm{\phi}||.

Hence in the following calculations we focus on the least multiplicative term
at the same differential order in the absence of ambiguity.
Moreover, because we make the same bootstrap assumption for connections and curvatures
as in paper
[
8
,
15
]
, so we focus on the terms contain matter fields
\phi
and
\zeta
.

4.1
L^{2}(\mathcal{S})
estimates of connection coefficients

We start with looking at the estimates for the connection
coefficients.

Proposition 4
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1,\quad\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+1.

Here

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]\equiv\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}=\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}.

Proof.

Make use of (
43k
) and (
43j
) for
\lambda
and
\sigma
respectively.
Pure gravitation effect and leads to the same analysis in the vacuum or scalar case,
see
[
8
,
15
]
.
∎

Proposition 5
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{2}]+1.

Proof.

Make use of (
43c
) and the definition of
{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}
we have

\displaystyle\textit{þ}{\underline{\omega}}=

\displaystyle 2\tau\bar{\tau}+2\tau\pi+2\bar{\tau}\bar{\pi}+\Psi_{2}+\bar{\Psi}_{2}+2\mathrm{i}(\bar{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}\phi_{0}-{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}\bar{\phi}_{0}+\bar{\zeta}_{1}\phi_{1}-\zeta_{1}\bar{\phi}_{1})

and for
k\leq 10
, commuting
\textit{ð}^{k}
with
þ
, we obtain

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\textit{ð}^{k}{\underline{\omega}}=

\displaystyle\textit{ð}^{k}\Psi_{2}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k,i_{3}<k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\Psi_{2}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma)\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,{\underline{\omega}})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{i}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\zeta_{j}.

Then we have

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\textit{þ}\textit{ð}^{k}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Vac+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\phi_{i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\zeta_{j}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{2}]+\frac{1}{|u|}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}+1\leq\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{2}]+1.

Here "Vac" means the vacuum case which contains curvatures and connections, analysis can be
found in
[
8
,
15
]
.
In what follows, we use "Vac" to denote the same object and present the results directly,
omitting the details of the analysis.

∎

Proposition 6
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\underline{\varrho}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[\Psi_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]^{2}+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]+1.

Proof.

Make use of

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}{\underline{\varrho}}

\displaystyle=\Psi_{2}+\bar{\Psi}_{2}+{\underline{\varrho}}\,{\underline{\omega}}+2\,\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{4}\,\phi_{0}-2\,\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{4}\,\bar{\phi}_{0}+2\,\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{1}\,\phi_{1}-2\,\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{1}\,\bar{\phi}_{1}

\displaystyle\quad+2\,\pi\,\bar{\pi}+2\,\pi\,\tau+2\,\bar{\pi}\,\bar{\tau}\,.

Commuting
\textit{þ}^{\prime}
with
\textit{ð}^{k}
, we have that

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\textit{ð}^{k}{\underline{\varrho}}+k\mu\textit{ð}^{k}{\underline{\varrho}}=\textit{ð}^{k}\Psi_{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\textit{ð}^{i}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)\textit{ð}^{k-i}\pi+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\textit{ð}^{i}{\underline{\omega}}\textit{ð}^{k-i}{\underline{\chi}}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\textit{ð}^{i}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{k-i}\zeta_{l}.

Denote the righthand side of the above equation by
F
. One has that

\displaystyle s_{2}({\underline{\varrho}})=0,\ \ s_{2}(\textit{ð}^{k}{\underline{\varrho}})=\frac{k}{2},\ \ s_{2}(F)=\frac{k+2}{2},

\displaystyle\lambda_{0}=-k,\ \ \lambda_{1}=-\lambda_{0}-1=k-1,

\displaystyle\lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(F)=-3,\ \ \lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(\textit{ð}^{k}{\underline{\varrho}})=-1.

Now, using Prop.
2
, one obtains

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}{\underline{\varrho}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\textit{ð}^{k}\Psi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\textit{ð}^{i}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{k-i}\Gamma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\textit{ð}^{i}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{k-i}\zeta_{l}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Vac+I.

For Vac part we have control
\frac{\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[\Psi_{2}]}{|u|}
.
For the integral
I
, we have

\displaystyle I\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\phi[\phi_{0}]\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\zeta[\zeta_{4}]+\frac{1}{|u|}\lesssim\frac{\phi[\phi_{0}]\zeta[\zeta_{4}]+1}{|u|}.

Hence we have

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}{\underline{\varrho}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[\Psi_{2}]+\phi[\phi_{0}]\zeta[\zeta_{4}]+1

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[\Psi_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]^{2}+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]+1.

Here in the last step we make use of the estimate for
\phi_{0}
and
\zeta_{4}
which will be
shown in Prop.
11
and Prop.
12
.
∎

Proposition 7
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+\Psi[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+1.

Proof.

We make use of the structure equation (
43a
)

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\tau

\displaystyle=(\tau+\bar{\pi})\rho+(\bar{\tau}+\pi)\sigma+{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}+2\mathrm{i}(2\bar{\zeta}_{1}\phi_{0}-\zeta_{3}\bar{\phi}_{0}-\zeta_{0}\bar{\phi}_{1}).

For
k\leq 10
, commuting
\textit{ð}^{k}
with
þ
, we have that

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\textit{ð}^{k}\tau=Vac+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\zeta_{i}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{l}.

Then we have

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\textit{þ}\textit{ð}^{k}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+1+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{2}+\frac{a}{|u|}\Gamma[\zeta_{0}]\Gamma[\phi_{1}]\leq\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+\zeta[\zeta_{0}]\phi[\phi_{1}]+1

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+\Psi[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+1.

∎

Proposition 8
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]^{2}+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]+1.

Proof.

We make use of the structure equation for
\rho
(
43h
)

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\rho

\displaystyle=\rho^{2}+\sigma\bar{\sigma}+2\mathrm{i}(\bar{\zeta}_{0}\phi_{0}-\zeta_{0}\bar{\phi}_{0}).

For
k\leq 10
, commuting
\textit{ð}^{k}
with
þ
, we have that

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\textit{ð}^{k}\rho=

\displaystyle Vac+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{0}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\zeta_{0}.

Then we have

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\textit{þ}\textit{ð}^{k}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{a}{|u|}\left({\bm{\Gamma}}(\sigma)_{\infty}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\sigma)_{2}+{\bm{\Gamma}}(\phi_{0})_{\infty}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\zeta_{0})_{2}+{\bm{\Gamma}}(\zeta_{0})_{\infty}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\phi_{0})_{2}\right)+\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|}+\frac{a\mathcal{O}}{|u|^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{a}{|u|}(\Gamma[\sigma]^{2}+\zeta[\zeta_{0}]\phi[\phi_{0}])

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}\left(\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]^{2}+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]\right)+1.

The initial value of
\rho
is
-\frac{1}{|u|}
which is independent of spherical coordinates,
hence when
1\leq k\leq 10
,

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}=0

and then when
1\leq k\leq 10

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}\left(\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]^{2}+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]\right).

∎

Proposition 9
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{2}]+1,

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1.

Proof.

We make use of the structure equation (
18b
) for
{\tilde{\mu}}

and the definition of
{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}
and obtain

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\tilde{\mu}+2\mu\tilde{\mu}=\tilde{\mu}^{2}-{\underline{\omega}}\mu-\lambda\bar{\lambda}+2\mathrm{i}({\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}\bar{\phi}_{1}-\bar{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}\phi_{1}).

Commuting
\textit{ð}^{k}
with
\textit{þ}^{\prime}
, we find that

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\textit{ð}^{k}{\tilde{\mu}}+(k+2)\mu\textit{ð}^{k}{\tilde{\mu}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{k}\textit{ð}^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)\textit{ð}^{k-i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\textit{ð}^{i}{\underline{\omega}}\textit{ð}^{k-i}\mu+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\textit{ð}^{i}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}\textit{ð}^{k-i}\phi_{1}

which leads to the following estimate:

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Vac+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\phi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 1+\frac{a}{|u|}\Gamma[{\underline{\omega}}]+\frac{1}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}]\phi[\phi_{1}]\leq 1+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{2}].

Note here for
{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}
we can only obtain its tenth derivative on the ingoing lightcone and
hence we translate the estimate from
\mathcal{S}
to
\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u)
.
The same strategy is used for
\pi
in the next proposition.
With the relation between
\mu
and
{\tilde{\mu}}
we have

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1.

∎

Proposition 10
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+1.

Proof.

We make use of the structure equation (
43b
) for
\pi

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\pi=-(\pi+\bar{\tau})\mu-(\bar{\pi}+\tau)\lambda-{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}-2\mathrm{i}(-{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}\bar{\phi}_{0}+2\bar{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}\phi_{1}-\zeta_{2}\bar{\phi}_{1}).

Commuting
\textit{ð}^{k}
with
\textit{þ}^{\prime}
, we find that

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\textit{ð}^{k}\pi+(k+1)\mu\textit{ð}^{k}\pi=

\displaystyle-\textit{ð}^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}-\mu\textit{ð}^{k}\bar{\tau}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\textit{ð}^{i}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)\textit{ð}^{k-i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\textit{ð}^{i}\bar{\zeta}_{j}\textit{ð}^{k-i}\phi_{l}.

We then obtain the following estimate

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle Vac+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\textit{ð}^{i}\bar{\zeta}_{j}\textit{ð}^{k-i}\phi_{l}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+\bm{\Gamma}[\tau]}{|u|}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\phi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]}{|u|}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\phi[\phi_{0}]\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}]}{|u|^{2}}+\frac{\mathcal{O}^{2}}{|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]}{|u|}.

∎

4.2
L^{2}(\mathcal{S})
estimates of
\phi_{A}

Proposition 11
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\phi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\phi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+1.

Proof.

We make use of equation of motion for
\phi_{0}
:

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\phi_{0}-\textit{ð}\phi_{1}

\displaystyle=(\frac{{\underline{\omega}}}{2}-\mu)\phi_{0}-\frac{\bar{\tau}\phi_{1}}{2}.

Commuting
\textit{ð}^{k}
with
\textit{þ}^{\prime}
, we find that

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{0}+(k+1)\mu\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{0}=

\displaystyle-\textit{ð}^{k+1}\phi_{1}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\textit{ð}^{i}\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},{\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)\textit{ð}^{k-i}\phi_{0}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\textit{ð}^{i}\tau\textit{ð}^{k-i}\phi_{1}.

Denoting the righthand side of the previous equation by
F
, one has that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\phi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\phi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\phi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},{\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\phi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\tau\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\phi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 1+\underline{{\bm{\phi}}}[\phi_{1}]+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 1+\underline{{\bm{\phi}}}[\phi_{1}]+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{2}.

Make use of

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\phi_{1}=\phi_{0}(\pi-\frac{\bar{\pi}}{2})+\phi_{1}\rho+\textit{ð}^{\prime}\phi_{0}

and obtain

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{1}=\textit{ð}^{k+1}\phi_{0}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\pi,\rho)\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}(\phi_{0},\phi_{1}).

We then have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\phi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\phi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\textit{þ}\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\phi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\phi_{0},\phi_{1})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 1+{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+\frac{1}{|u|}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}\lesssim 1+{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}].

∎

Proposition 12
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\zeta_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\zeta_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{2}],

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{3}],\quad\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\zeta_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}],

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\zeta_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]+{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\zeta_{5}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{4}].

For
0\leq k\leq 9
, one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\zeta_{3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+1,

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1,\quad\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\zeta_{5}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}].

Proof.

We start with
\zeta_{0}
and make use of (
39a
)

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\zeta_{0}+\mu\zeta_{0}-\textit{ð}\zeta_{1}=\Gamma\zeta_{j}+\zeta_{j}\phi_{l},

and obtain

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{0}+(k+1)\mu\zeta_{0}=

\displaystyle\textit{ð}^{k+1}\zeta_{1}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\textit{ð}^{i}\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},{\tilde{\mu}},\lambda,\rho,\sigma,\tau)\textit{ð}^{k-i}\zeta_{0,1,2,3,4}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\phi_{j_{1}}\textit{ð}^{i_{2}}\phi_{j_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\zeta_{j_{3}}.

Denoting the righthand side of the previous equation by
F
, one has that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\zeta_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\zeta_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\zeta_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\zeta_{j}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\phi_{j_{1}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\phi_{j_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\zeta_{j_{3}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 1+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 1+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{2}+\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\mathcal{O}^{3}\leq 1+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}].

Similarly for
\zeta_{1}
we make use of the ingoing equation (
39c
) and obtain

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\zeta_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{2}].

Next we estimate
{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}
and we make use of (
42a
)

\displaystyle\textit{þ}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}=\textit{ð}^{\prime}\zeta_{3}+(\Psi_{2}+\textit{ð}\pi)\phi_{0}+\phi_{0}\mu\rho+\zeta_{i}\phi_{j}^{2}+\Gamma\zeta_{i}+\Gamma^{2}\phi_{j}.

For term
(\Psi_{2}+\textit{ð}\pi)\phi_{0}
and
\phi_{0}\mu\rho
we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}||\phi_{0}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{i}(\Psi_{2}+\textit{ð}\pi)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\Psi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{a}{|u|}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{i}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i+1}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}({\bm{\Psi}}[\Psi_{2}]+\Gamma[\pi])+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}[\pi]\lesssim 1.

Here the definition of
{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}[\pi]
will be introduced in
5.1
.

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{i}(\phi_{0}\mu\rho)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\lesssim\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{|u|^{2}}{a}\mathcal{O}^{3}\lesssim 1.

Then we obtain that for
0\leq k\leq 10
the following holds

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{3}].

By definition of
{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}
we have

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\zeta_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}(\phi_{0}\mu)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\frac{1}{|u|}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\phi[\phi_{0}]\frac{|u|^{2}}{a}\Gamma[\mu]\lesssim 1+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}].

For
\zeta_{2}
we make use of its outgoing equation (
39d
)

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\zeta_{2}=\textit{ð}^{\prime}\zeta_{1}+\Gamma(\lambda,\rho,\sigma,\pi)\zeta_{0,1,2,4}+\zeta_{j}\phi_{l}^{2}.

Then we can compute

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{2}=\textit{ð}^{k+1}\zeta_{1}+\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\pi,\tau)^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\zeta_{l}+\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{5}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\pi,\tau)^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\zeta_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{j_{1}}\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\phi_{j_{2}},

and obtain

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\zeta_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{1}]+\zeta[\zeta_{4}]\Gamma[\sigma]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]+{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{1}]+1.

For
\zeta_{3}
we first estimate its derivative up to 9 by using (
42b
),

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\zeta_{3}+2\mu\zeta_{3}=\textit{ð}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}-\phi_{0}\textit{ð}\mu-\phi_{1}\mu\sigma+\zeta_{i}\phi_{j}^{2}+\Gamma\zeta_{i}+\Gamma^{2}\phi_{j},

and obtain

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{\leq 9}\zeta_{3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{\leq 9}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{|u|^{2}}{a}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\phi[\phi_{1}]\Gamma[\mu]\Gamma[\sigma]+\frac{\mathcal{O}^{3}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 1+\frac{a}{|u|}\phi[\phi_{1}]\Gamma[\mu]\Gamma[\sigma]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+1.

We leave the analysis of its 10 derivative into the elliptic part; see Remark
5
.

For
{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}
we make use of (
22b
),

\displaystyle\textit{þ}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}=\textit{ð}^{\prime}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}+2\Psi_{2}\phi_{1}+2\phi_{1}\textit{ð}\pi-\phi_{0}\textit{ð}^{\prime}\mu+\zeta_{i}\phi_{j}^{2}+\Gamma\zeta_{i}+\Gamma^{2}\phi_{j},

and estimate its derivative up to 9.
The reason cannot to 10 is we cannot obtain the 11 derivative of
{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}
on the light cone.
Follow the similar analysis we obtain

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{\leq 9}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\zeta[{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}]+\frac{1}{a}\phi[\phi_{0}]\Gamma[{\tilde{\mu}}]+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}^{2}\lesssim 1.

With such results we control
\zeta_{5}
to 9 derivative by the definition of
{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}
:

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{\leq 9}\zeta_{5}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{\leq 9}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{\leq 9}(\phi_{1}\mu)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\frac{1}{|u|}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\phi[\phi_{1}]\frac{|u|^{2}}{a}\Gamma[\mu]\lesssim 1+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}].

For the 10 derivative of
\zeta_{5}
we make use of (
39h
),

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\zeta_{5}=\textit{ð}^{\prime}\zeta_{4}-\zeta_{1}\mu+\zeta_{i}\phi_{j}^{2}+\Gamma\zeta_{i}+\Gamma^{2}\phi_{j},

and obtain

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\zeta_{5}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{|u|}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k+1}\zeta_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{4}].

∎

Proposition 13
.

For
0\leq k\leq 9
, one has that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1,

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{1,2,3,4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle{\bm{\Psi}}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]+1

and

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}K||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1.

where
K
is the Gaussian curvature.

Proof.

For
\Psi_{0}
, we make use of (
44
),

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\Psi_{0}+\mu\Psi_{0}=\textit{ð}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}+2\mathrm{i}(\phi_{0}\textit{ð}\bar{\zeta}_{1}-\bar{\phi}_{0}\textit{ð}\zeta_{3})+\zeta_{i}^{2}+\zeta_{i}\phi_{j}^{3}+\Psi_{i}\phi_{j}^{2}+\zeta_{i}\phi_{j}\Gamma+\phi_{j}^{2}\Gamma^{2}+\Gamma\Psi_{i},

and obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\underline{{\bm{\Psi}}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{2}\lesssim 1.

For
\Psi_{II}=\Psi_{1,2,3,4}
we make use of their outgoing equation (
48
), (
49
),
(
50
), (
A.4
):

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\Psi_{II}=\textit{ð}^{\prime}(\Psi_{0},{\tilde{\Psi}})+\phi_{j}\textit{ð}\zeta_{0,...,4}+\zeta_{i}^{2}+\zeta_{i}\phi_{j}^{3}+\Psi_{i}\phi_{j}^{2}+\zeta_{i}\phi_{j}\Gamma+\phi_{j}^{2}\Gamma^{2}+\Gamma\Psi_{i}.

The large terms are
\mu\zeta_{0}\phi_{i}
which can be estimated in the following:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mu\textit{ð}^{i}\zeta_{0}\textit{ð}^{k-i}\phi_{j}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\frac{|u|^{2}}{a}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\zeta[\zeta_{0}]a^{\frac{1}{2}}\phi[\phi_{j}]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]+1.

According to the results in vacuum case we obtain

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\Psi_{II}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle{\bm{\Psi}}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]+1.

For the analysis of Gaussian curvature,
one define the complex Gaussian curvature:

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}\equiv

\displaystyle\Lambda+\Phi_{11}-\Psi_{2}+\mu\rho-\lambda\sigma

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{i}\bar{\zeta}_{1}\phi_{1}-\mathrm{i}\zeta_{1}\bar{\phi}_{1}+\mathrm{i}\bar{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}\phi_{0}-\mathrm{i}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}\bar{\phi}_{0}-\Psi_{2}+\mu\rho-\lambda\sigma

where the Gaussian curvature is
K=\mathcal{K}+\bar{\mathcal{K}}
.
The initial data of
\mathcal{K}
on the ingoing cone
\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{\star}
is
\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}
,
we anticipate that Gaussian curvature keep such property,
hence considering the signature,
we has that the bootstrap assumption for
\mathcal{K}
is

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\mathcal{K}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\mathcal{O}.

Make use of its’ ingoing equation

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\mathcal{K}=

\displaystyle-2\mu\mathcal{K}-\textit{ð}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}+\mathrm{i}(\phi_{1}\textit{ð}^{\prime}\bar{\zeta}_{2}+\bar{\phi}_{0}\textit{ð}\zeta_{5}-\bar{\phi}_{1}\textit{ð}\zeta_{2}-\phi_{1}\textit{ð}\bar{\zeta}_{4})

\displaystyle+\mu\textit{ð}^{\prime}\tau-\lambda\textit{ð}\tau+\zeta_{i}^{2}+\zeta_{i}\phi_{j}^{3}+\Psi_{i}\phi_{j}^{2}+\zeta_{i}\phi_{j}\Gamma+\phi_{j}^{2}\Gamma^{2}+\Gamma\Psi_{i}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\textit{ð}^{k}\mathcal{K}+(k+2)\mu\textit{ð}^{k}\mathcal{K}=

\displaystyle-\textit{ð}^{k+1}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\textit{ð}^{i}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{k+1-i}\zeta_{l}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\textit{ð}^{i}\Gamma(\mu,\lambda)\textit{ð}^{k+1-i}\tau

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\textit{ð}^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)\textit{ð}^{k-i}\mathcal{K}+\textit{ð}^{k}(rest).

We denote the right hand side by
F
and
apply the ingoing transport inequality, one has

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\mathcal{K}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\mathcal{K}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}.

Apply the results and the bootstrap assumptions, one has

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\mathcal{K}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1.

∎

4.3
Summary of
L^{2}

Under the bootstrap assumption

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}},{\bm{\phi}},{\bm{\zeta}},{\bm{\Psi}}\leq\mathcal{O},

we obtain the following improvement for
{\bm{\Gamma}}
:
For
0\leq k\leq 10
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1,\quad\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+1,

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{2}]+1,\quad||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+1.

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+\Psi[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\zeta_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+1,\quad||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\zeta_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{2}]+1,

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{3}],\quad\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\zeta_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}],

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\zeta_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]+{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\zeta_{5}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{4}].

For
0\leq k\leq 9
, we have

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\zeta_{3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+1,

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1,\quad\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\zeta_{5}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}].

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1,

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\Psi_{1,2,3,4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle{\bm{\Psi}}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}K||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1.

Remark 4
.

Actually, we also anticipate that the derivative of
\mathcal{K}
has "better" property
by eliminating
\frac{a}{|u|}
which comes from the initial data.
When
1\leq k\leq 9
, we can make use of results for
\rho
,
\mu
,
\lambda
,
\sigma
,
\Psi_{2}
,

\phi_{j}
,
\zeta_{1}
and
{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}
obtained.
For those terms under next-to-leading order derivative,
we have better control.

For the k derivative of
\zeta_{1}
and
\phi_{1}
where
0\leq k\leq 10
, i.e. the next to leading order derivative, the norm on the light cone can be bounded by its norm on
\mathcal{S}
, we have

\displaystyle\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]_{k}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}}{a}\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]_{k}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\phi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim 1.

Same analysis leads to

\displaystyle\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{2}]_{k}=

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{1}]_{k}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]_{k}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\phi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim 1.

such results lead to

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k\leq 9}\Psi_{1,2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1,\quad||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k\leq 9}\zeta_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1,\quad\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k\leq 9}\zeta_{5}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1.

Moreover for the derivative of
\Psi_{0}
up to 9 derivative we have

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]_{k}\lesssim 1

which leads to

\displaystyle\Gamma[\sigma]_{0\leq k\leq 9}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1,\quad\Gamma[\rho]_{0}\lesssim 1,\Gamma[\rho]_{1\leq k\leq 9}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|},

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{0\leq k\leq 9}\zeta_{2,3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1,

Then from the definition of the Gaussian curvature we can calculate directly and obtain:

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{7}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}K||_{L_{sc}^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\sum_{i=1}^{9}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}K||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1.

5
Elliptic

In this section we estimate the top-derivative, i.e. the 11th derivative of connections
which are needed in the energy estimate for curvature as well as the matter fields.
For the purpose of closing the bootstrap argument, we sidestep top-order Weyl curvature estimates.
The strategy is to construct new quantities made of the divergence or the curl of connections.
Then the elliptic inequalities lead to that the estimate of higher derivative can be obtained
from the lower derivative of such quantities.
By doing so, one can avoid the estimate of 11-derivative of curvature.
We start with introducing the basic elliptic inequalities in the scale-invariant norms:

Proposition 14
.

Let
f
denote a quantity with nonzero
T-weight. Then under the
bootstrap assumption, for
0\leq k\leq 11
, one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}f||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\sum_{i\leq k-1}\left(||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i+1}\mathcal{D}^{i}\mathscr{D}_{f}||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}f||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\right).

The proof can be found in Sec 6.1 in Paper
[
9
]
.

When
f
is a pure scalar, we have the following:

Proposition 15
.

Let
f
denote a quantity with zero T-weight. Then, under the
bootstrap assumption, for
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k+1}f||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S})}\lesssim||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k+1}\mathcal{D}^{k-1}({\bm{\Delta}}f)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S})}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}f||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S})},

where
{\bm{\Delta}}f\equiv 2\textit{ð}\textit{ð}^{\prime}f
.

With the Prop.
14
we have the following remark:

Remark 5
.

From the equation (
40a
) which gives the relation between
\textit{ð}^{\prime}\zeta_{3}
and
\textit{ð}\zeta_{1}
,
we have

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\zeta_{3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i\leq k-1}\left(||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i+1}\mathcal{D}^{i}\textit{ð}^{\prime}\zeta_{3}||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\zeta_{3}||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\zeta_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\phi[\phi_{0,1}]\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1,2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\zeta[\zeta_{4}]\Gamma[\rho]+\zeta[\zeta_{2}]\Gamma[\sigma]+\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\zeta_{1,3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}.

When
k=10
, apply the estimate in previous we have

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}\zeta_{3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}\zeta_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+1\lesssim 1+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{2}].

For the norm on the light cone, we have

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}\lesssim

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+1,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}+1.

The above results show the equivalence of the norm between
\zeta_{3}
and
\zeta_{1}
.

5.1
Elliptic estimates for 11 derivative

In this section we estimate the 11-derivative of connections.
Again we focus on terms contain matter field.
The norm of the eleventh derivative quantities of the connection coefficients is defined as

\displaystyle\bm{\Gamma}_{11}(u,v)\equiv

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}

\displaystyle+||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}+||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}

\displaystyle+\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\lambda||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\mu||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

In the following estimates, we make use of the
L^{2}(\mathcal{S})
estimates and
the bootstrap assumptions

\displaystyle\bm{\phi},\bm{\zeta},\bm{\Psi},\bm{\Gamma}_{11}\leq\mathcal{O},

We will show that

{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}^{2}+\bm{\Psi}+\bm{\phi}^{2}+\bm{\phi}+{\bm{\zeta}}^{2}+{\bm{\zeta}}+1.

Proposition 16
.

One has that

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]+\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}\lesssim

\displaystyle\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]+\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+1,

and

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}\lesssim\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]+\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+1.

Proof.

For
\rho
we make use of (
43h
)

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\,\rho

\displaystyle=2\,\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{0}\,\phi_{0}-2\,\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{0}\,\bar{\phi}_{0}+\rho^{2}+\sigma\,\bar{\sigma},

and focus on the matter field terms
\zeta_{0}\phi_{0}
and have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\bar{\phi}_{0}\textit{ð}^{11}\zeta_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\leq

\displaystyle\phi[\phi_{0}]\int_{0}^{v}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\zeta_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}\phi[\phi_{0}]\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\int_{0}^{v}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\zeta_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}\phi[\phi_{0}]{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{0}]\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{0}],

and similarly we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\bar{\zeta}_{0}\textit{ð}^{11}\phi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\leq

\displaystyle\zeta[\zeta_{0}]\int_{0}^{v}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\phi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}\zeta[\zeta_{0}]\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}].

Then we have

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{a}{|u|}\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]+\frac{a}{|u|}{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{0}].

(30)

Then make use of
\textit{ð}\rho-\textit{ð}^{\prime}\sigma=\tau\rho-\bar{\tau}\sigma-{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}
,
here
\textit{ð}^{\prime}\sigma=\mathscr{D}_{\sigma}
and the elliptic inequality we have

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i\leq 10}(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\mathscr{D}_{\sigma}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\sum_{i=0}^{10}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+1

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\sum_{i=0}^{10}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]+{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{0}]+1

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{10}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]+{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{0}]+1.

Integrating along the light cone one obtains the results.

∎

Proposition 17
.

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{2}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+\underline{{\bm{\phi}}}[\phi_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[\Psi_{2}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+\underline{{\bm{\phi}}}[\phi_{1}]+1.

Proof.

Define

\displaystyle\tilde{\tau}\equiv\textit{ð}^{\prime}\tau-\Psi_{2},

we can obtain the equation for
\tilde{\tau}
(
52
):

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\tilde{\tau}=2\mathrm{i}(\bar{\zeta}_{0}\phi_{0}\mu-\zeta_{0}\bar{\phi}_{0}\mu+\phi_{0}\textit{ð}^{\prime}\bar{\zeta}_{1}-\bar{\phi}_{0}\textit{ð}\zeta_{1})+\zeta_{i}\phi_{j}^{3}+\zeta_{i}^{2}+\Psi\phi_{j}^{2}+\Gamma\zeta_{i}\phi_{j}+\Gamma\textit{ð}\Gamma+\Gamma\Psi+\Gamma^{3}

where we list the leading terms and top-derivative terms and then obtain

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{10}\tilde{\tau}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle{\bm{\Psi}}[\Psi_{0}]+\zeta[\zeta_{0}]\phi[\phi_{0}]+\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\phi_{0}\textit{ð}^{11}\bar{\zeta}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+1+\frac{\mathcal{O}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle{\bm{\Psi}}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+\underline{{\bm{\phi}}}[\phi_{1}]+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\Gamma[\phi_{0}]\int_{0}^{v}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\zeta_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+1+\frac{\mathcal{O}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle{\bm{\Psi}}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1}]+\underline{{\bm{\phi}}}[\phi_{1}]+1.

6
Energy Estimate

In this section we show the energy estimate

\displaystyle\bm{\phi}+{\bm{\zeta}}+\Psi\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}+1

to close the bootstrap argument.
To achieve this goal, we make use of the energy inequality in scale-invariant norm.
We introduce in the following: let

(\Psi_{I},\Psi_{II})\in\{(\Psi_{0},\tilde{\Psi}_{1}),(\tilde{\Psi}_{1},\Psi_{2}),(\Psi_{2},\tilde{\Psi}_{3}),(\tilde{\Psi}_{3},\Psi_{4}),(\phi_{0},\phi_{1}),(\zeta_{0},\zeta_{1}),(\zeta_{1},\zeta_{2}),({\tilde{\zeta}}_{4},{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}),(\zeta_{4},\zeta_{5})\}.

the Bianchi identities and the equations for the
scalar field take the schematic form

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\Psi_{I}+\lambda_{0}\mu\Psi_{I}-\textit{ð}\Psi_{II}=P_{0},

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\Psi_{II}-\textit{ð}^{\prime}\Psi_{I}=Q_{0}.

Applying the derivative
ð
and the commutator relations it
follows that

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\textit{ð}^{k}\Psi_{I}+(\lambda_{0}+k)\mu\textit{ð}^{k}\Psi_{I}-\textit{ð}^{k+1}\Psi_{II}=P_{k},

(34a)

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\textit{ð}^{k}\Psi_{II}-\textit{ð}^{\prime}\textit{ð}^{k}\Psi_{I}=Q_{k},

(34b)

where

\displaystyle P_{k}=\textit{ð}^{k}P_{0}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\textit{ð}^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)\textit{ð}^{k-i}\Psi_{I}

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\pi,\tau)^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\Psi_{I}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\pi,\tau)^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}Q_{0}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma)\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{II}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}K\textit{ð}^{i_{2}}\Psi_{I}.

we have that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}|u|^{2\lambda_{0}-4s_{2}(\Psi_{I})-2}||\textit{ð}^{k}\Psi_{I}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}a|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{0}-4s_{2}(\Psi_{I})-4}||\textit{ð}^{k}\Psi_{II}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

0\leq k\leq 11
, one has that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\phi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\phi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\leq\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{4}}}.

Proof.

For
(\phi_{0},\phi_{1})
we make use of the equations

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\phi_{0}-\textit{ð}\phi_{1}

\displaystyle=(\frac{{\underline{\omega}}}{2}-\mu)\phi_{0}-\frac{\bar{\tau}\phi_{1}}{2},

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\phi_{1}-\textit{ð}^{\prime}\phi_{0}

\displaystyle=\phi_{0}(\pi-\frac{\bar{\pi}}{2})+\phi_{1}\rho,

and the energy inequality (
35
), then we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\phi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v))}+\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\phi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u))}+\frac{1}{a}(M+N),

(37)

where

\displaystyle M\equiv

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}P_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})},

\displaystyle N\equiv

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}Q_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

and

\displaystyle P_{k}=

\displaystyle\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},\tau,\lambda)\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{0,1}+\phi_{0,1}\textit{ð}^{k}\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},\tau,{\tilde{\mu}})+\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},{\underline{\omega}},\tau,\lambda)\textit{ð}^{k-i}(\phi_{0},\phi_{1}),

\displaystyle Q_{k}=

\displaystyle\Gamma(\rho,\sigma,\pi,\tau)\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{0,1}+\phi_{0,1}\textit{ð}^{k}\Gamma(\pi,\rho)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{3}+i_{4}<k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma)\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}(\phi_{0},\phi_{1})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}K\textit{ð}^{i_{2}}\phi_{0}.

For
M
we have

\displaystyle M\leq

\displaystyle 2\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u^{\prime}}(0,v))}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}J^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]J^{\frac{1}{2}},

where

\displaystyle J\equiv\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

Then substitute
P_{k}
, we make use of the estimate in the elliptic part to estimate
the top derivative of connections

\displaystyle J\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\phi_{0,1}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},{\tilde{\mu}},\tau,\lambda)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},\lambda,\tau)(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\phi_{0,1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{i}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i})\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},{\tilde{\mu}},\tau,\lambda)(a^{\frac{k-1-i}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k-i})\phi_{0,1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}.

Then we have

\displaystyle J_{1}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},{\tilde{\mu}},\tau)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2}}{a}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},\tau)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\mu}})||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\lambda)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}(\bm{\phi}+\underline{\bm{\phi}}+\bm{\zeta}+\underline{\bm{\zeta}}+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+1)^{2}+1,

\displaystyle J_{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\left(\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\Gamma[\lambda]^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\phi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\Gamma[\tau]^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\phi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]^{2}+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma[\tau]^{2}\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]^{2},

\displaystyle J_{3}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\Gamma[\lambda]a\phi[\phi_{0,1}]\lesssim\frac{1}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{4}.

Then collect the results one obtain

\displaystyle J\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]^{2}+1+\frac{\mathcal{O}^{4}}{a},

and then

\displaystyle M\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]J^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|}\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]^{2}+\frac{a}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]+\mathcal{O}^{2}\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}].

For N we have

\displaystyle N\leq

\displaystyle 2\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}Q_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left(\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v^{\prime}}(u_{\infty},u))}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}H^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]H^{\frac{1}{2}},

where

\displaystyle H\equiv\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}Q_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

Substituting the definition of
Q_{k}
into
H
we obtain

\displaystyle H\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\phi_{0,1}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Gamma(\rho,\sigma,\pi)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\Gamma(\rho,\sigma,\pi,\tau)(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\phi_{0,1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{3}+i_{4}<k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\sigma,...)\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{0,1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}K\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\phi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}=H_{1}+...+H_{4}.

We have

\displaystyle H_{1}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Gamma(\rho,\sigma,\pi)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2}}{a}\bm{\Gamma}_{11}[\rho,\pi]^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{a}(\bm{\phi}+\underline{\bm{\phi}}+\bm{\zeta}+\underline{\bm{\zeta}}+\bm{\Psi}+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}+1)^{2},

\displaystyle H_{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}(\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\Gamma[\sigma]^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\phi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\Gamma[\pi]^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\phi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{2}}(\bm{\Psi}+1)^{2}\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]^{2}+\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}(\bm{\Psi}+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}+1)^{2}\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]^{2},

\displaystyle H_{3}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a\Gamma[\sigma]^{2}a\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{a}\mathcal{O}^{4},

\displaystyle H_{4}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}K||^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-1-i}\phi_{0}||^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\phi[\phi_{0}]^{2}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-1}K||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim
