\displaystyle\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\phi[\phi_{0}]^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-1}K||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}.

Then collect the results we have

\displaystyle H\leq

\displaystyle\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{2}}(\bm{\Psi}+1)^{2}\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]^{2}+\frac{\mathcal{O}^{4}}{a},

and hence we have

\displaystyle N\lesssim a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]H^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|}(\bm{\Psi}+1)\bm{\phi}\underline{\bm{\phi}}+\mathcal{O}^{2}\bm{\phi}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{0}^{2}+\frac{1}{a}(\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|}\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]^{2}+\frac{a}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]+\mathcal{O}^{2}\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]+\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|}(\bm{\Psi}+1)\bm{\phi}\underline{\bm{\phi}}+\mathcal{O}^{2}\bm{\phi})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{0}^{2}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{4}}}.

∎

6.2
Energy estimate for
\zeta_{ABA^{\prime}}

Proposition 22
.

For
0\leq k\leq 11
, one has that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\leq\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{4}}}.

Proof.

For pair
(\zeta_{0},\zeta_{1})
, we make use of

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\zeta_{0}-\textit{ð}\,\zeta_{1}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{4}\,\phi_{0}^{2}-\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{4}\,\phi_{0}\,\bar{\phi}_{0}-\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{1}\,\phi_{0}\,\phi_{1}+\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{3}\,\bar{\phi}_{0}\,\phi_{1}-\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{1}\,\phi_{0}\,\bar{\phi}_{1}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{0}\,\phi_{1}\,\bar{\phi}_{1}-\zeta_{0}\,\mu+\frac{3\,\zeta_{0}\,{\underline{\omega}}}{2}+\zeta_{4}\,\rho+\zeta_{2}\,\sigma-\frac{5\,\zeta_{1}\,\tau}{2}-\zeta_{3}\,\bar{\tau},

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\zeta_{1}-\textit{ð}^{\prime}\,\zeta_{0}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{3}\,\phi_{0}^{2}+2\,\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{1}\,\phi_{0}\,\bar{\phi}_{0}+\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{0}\,\phi_{0}\,\phi_{1}-2\,\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{0}\,\bar{\phi}_{0}\,\phi_{1}+\zeta_{0}\,\pi+2\,\zeta_{1}\,\rho

\displaystyle\quad+\zeta_{3}\,\bar{\sigma}-\frac{3\,\zeta_{0}\,\bar{\tau}}{2}.

Applying
\textit{ð}^{k}
and commuting with
þ
and
\textit{þ}^{\prime}
we have that

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{0}+(k+1)\mu\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{0}-\textit{ð}^{k+1}\zeta_{1}=P_{k},

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{1}-\textit{ð}^{\prime}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{0}=Q_{k},

where

\displaystyle P_{k}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\phi_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\zeta_{m}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\zeta_{m},

\displaystyle Q_{k}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{5}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\zeta_{m}+\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\zeta_{m}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}K\textit{ð}^{i_{2}}\zeta_{0}.

Then apply the energy inequality (
35
) one has

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+\frac{1}{a}(M+N).

where

\displaystyle M\equiv

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}P_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})},

\displaystyle N\equiv

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}Q_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

For
M
we have

\displaystyle M\leq 2\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u^{\prime}}(0,v))}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}J^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{0}]J^{\frac{1}{2}},

where

\displaystyle J\equiv\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

Substitute
P_{k}
we have

\displaystyle J\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\phi_{0}^{2}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\phi_{0,1}^{2}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{0,1,3}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\phi_{0}\zeta_{4}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\phi_{0,1}\zeta_{0,1,3}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{0,1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\Gamma(\sigma,\lambda,...)(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{l}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\zeta_{m}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\Gamma||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k,i_{l}<k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\phi_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\zeta_{m}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k,i_{l}<k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\zeta_{m}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}=J_{1}+...+J_{8}.

We then have

\displaystyle J_{1}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}(a\phi[\phi_{0}]^{2})^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\leq\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{4}]^{2}.

Here the definition of
\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{4}]
is

\displaystyle\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{4}]\equiv||\frac{1}{|u|}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}=\left(\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

\displaystyle J_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}(a\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2})^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{0,1,3}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{a^{3}}{|u|^{4}}\frac{1}{a}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{1,3}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\frac{a^{4}}{|u|^{5}}\frac{1}{a}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\leq\frac{a^{3}}{|u|^{4}}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{1,3}]^{2}+\frac{a^{4}}{|u|^{5}}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{0}]^{2}.

\displaystyle J_{3}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}a\phi[\phi_{0}]^{2}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\zeta[\zeta_{4}]^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\phi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\phi[\phi_{0}]^{2}\zeta[\zeta_{4}]^{2}\frac{1}{a}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\phi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\phi[\phi_{0}]^{2}\zeta[\zeta_{4}]^{2}\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]^{2}.

\displaystyle J_{4}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}a\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2}(\zeta[\zeta_{1,3}]^{2},a\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2})||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\phi_{0,1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{4}}{|u|^{5}}\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2}\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2}\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]^{2}+\frac{a^{3}}{|u|^{4}}\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2}\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2}\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]^{2}.

\displaystyle J_{5}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}(||\Gamma(\lambda,{\underline{\omega}})(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||\sigma(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+||\tau(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{1,3}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||\rho(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big(\frac{a}{|u|}\Gamma[\lambda]^{2}+\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\Gamma[{\underline{\omega}}]^{2}\big)\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{0}]^{2}+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma[\sigma]^{2}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{2}]^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma[\tau]^{2}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{1,3}]^{2}+\frac{1}{|u|}\Gamma[\rho]^{2}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{4}]^{2}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{0}]^{2}+\frac{\mathcal{O}^{4}}{a}.

\displaystyle J_{6}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}(||\zeta_{0}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},{\underline{\omega}},\lambda)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||\zeta_{2}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\sigma||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+||\zeta_{1,3}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\tau||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||\zeta_{4}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\rho||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2}\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}[{\underline{\omega}}]^{2}+\frac{1}{a}\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2}\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}[{\tilde{\mu}}]^{2}+\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2}\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}[\lambda]^{2}+\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}\zeta[\zeta_{2}]^{2}\bm{\Gamma}_{11}[\sigma]^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\zeta[\zeta_{1,3}]^{2}\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}[\tau]^{2}+\frac{1}{|u|}\zeta[\zeta_{4}]^{2}\bm{\Gamma}_{11}[\rho]^{2}\lesssim 1+\frac{\mathcal{O}^{4}}{a}.

\displaystyle J_{7}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u|^{4}}\frac{|u|^{2}}{a}\zeta[\zeta_{4}]^{2}a^{2}\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{4}\lesssim\frac{1}{|u|^{3}}\mathcal{O}^{6}.

\displaystyle J_{8}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\mathcal{O}^{4}\lesssim\frac{1}{a|u|}\mathcal{O}^{4}.

Collect the results one has

\displaystyle J\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{0}]^{2}+1+\frac{\mathcal{O}^{6}}{a},

and then

\displaystyle M\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{0}]J^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{0}]^{2}+\frac{a}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{0}]+\mathcal{O}^{3}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{0}].

For
N
We have

\displaystyle N\leq 2\left(\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v^{\prime}}(u_{\infty},u))}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}H^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{1}]H^{\frac{1}{2}},

where

\displaystyle H\equiv\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}Q_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

Substitute
Q_{k}
we have

\displaystyle H\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\phi_{0,1}^{2}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{0,1,3}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\phi_{0,1}\zeta_{0,1,3}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{0,1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\Gamma(\sigma,...)(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{l}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\zeta_{m}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\Gamma||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{5}=k,i_{l}<k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\zeta_{m}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k,i_{l}<k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\zeta_{m}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}K\textit{ð}^{i_{2}}\zeta_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}=H_{1}+...+H_{7}.

From the analysis in
J
we have

\displaystyle H_{1}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{3}}{|u|^{4}}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{1,3}]^{2}+\frac{a^{4}}{|u|^{5}}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{0}]^{2},

\displaystyle H_{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{4}}{|u|^{5}}\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2}\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2}\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]^{2}+\frac{a^{3}}{|u|^{4}}\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2}\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2}\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{0}]^{2},

\displaystyle H_{3}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}(||\Gamma(\tau,\pi)(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||\Gamma(\rho,\sigma)(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{1,3}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\Gamma[\tau,\pi]^{2}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{0}]^{2}+\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma[\sigma]^{2}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{1,3}]^{2},

\displaystyle H_{4}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}(||\zeta_{0}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||\zeta_{1,3}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\Gamma(\rho,\sigma)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2}\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}[\tau,\pi]^{2}+\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}\zeta[\zeta_{1,3}]^{2}\bm{\Gamma}_{11}[\sigma]^{2},

\displaystyle H_{5}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}a\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2}a^{2}\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{4}\lesssim\frac{a^{3}}{|u|^{5}}\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2}\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{4},

\displaystyle H_{6}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a\mathcal{O}^{4}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}\mathcal{O}^{4},

\displaystyle H_{7}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}K||^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-1-i}\zeta_{0}||^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{4}}\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-1}K||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\lesssim\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-1}K||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}.

Collect the results one has

\displaystyle H\lesssim\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma[\sigma]^{2}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{1,3}]^{2}+\frac{\mathcal{O}^{6}}{a},

and then

\displaystyle N\lesssim a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{1}](\frac{a}{|u|}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{1}]+\frac{\mathcal{O}^{3}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}).

Combine with
M
one finally obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{4}}}.

∎

Proposition 23
.

For
0\leq k\leq 11
, one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\leq\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+1.

Proof.

For pair
(\zeta_{1},\zeta_{2})
we make use of

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\zeta_{1}-\textit{ð}\,\zeta_{2}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{5}\,\phi_{0}\,\bar{\phi}_{0}+\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{4}\,\phi_{0}\,\phi_{1}+\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{4}\,\bar{\phi}_{0}\,\phi_{1}-\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{1}\,\phi_{1}^{2}-\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{2}\,\phi_{0}\,\bar{\phi}_{1}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{1}\,\phi_{1}\,\bar{\phi}_{1}-2\,\zeta_{1}\,\mu+\frac{\zeta_{1}\,{\underline{\omega}}}{2}+\zeta_{5}\,\rho-\frac{\zeta_{2}\,\tau}{2}-\zeta_{4}\,\bar{\tau},

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\,\zeta_{2}-\textit{ð}^{\prime}\,\zeta_{1}

\displaystyle=2\,\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{2}\,\phi_{0}\,\bar{\phi}_{0}-\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{3}\,\phi_{0}\,\phi_{1}-2\,\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{1}\,\bar{\phi}_{0}\,\phi_{1}+\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{0}\,\phi_{1}^{2}-\zeta_{0}\,\lambda

\displaystyle\quad+2\,\zeta_{1}\,\pi+\zeta_{2}\,\rho+\zeta_{4}\,\bar{\sigma}-\frac{\zeta_{1}\,\tau}{2}\,.

Applying
\textit{ð}^{k}
and commuting with
þ
and
\textit{þ}^{\prime}
we have that

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{1}+(k+2)\mu\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{1}-\textit{ð}^{k+1}\zeta_{2}=P_{k},

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{2}-\textit{ð}^{\prime}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{1}=Q_{k},

where

\displaystyle P_{k}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\phi_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\zeta_{m}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\zeta_{m},

\displaystyle Q_{k}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{5}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\zeta_{m}+\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\zeta_{m}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}K\textit{ð}^{i_{2}}\zeta_{0}.

Then apply the energy inequality one has

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+(M+N),

where

\displaystyle M\equiv

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}P_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})},

\displaystyle N\equiv

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}Q_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

For
M
one need estimate

\displaystyle J\equiv\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

Compared with the analysis in pair
(\zeta_{0},\zeta_{1})
, we focus on the different terms.
For terms
\zeta_{5}\phi_{0}\bar{\phi}_{0}
and
\zeta_{2}\phi_{0}\bar{\phi}_{1}
which lead to the following:

\displaystyle J_{1}\equiv

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}||\phi_{0}^{2}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\phi_{0}\zeta_{5}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}||\phi_{0,1}^{2}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\phi_{0,1}\zeta_{2}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\phi_{0,1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k,i_{l}<k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\phi_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\zeta_{2,5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}=J_{11}+...+J_{15}.

One has the control

\displaystyle J_{11}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{4}}\phi[\phi_{0}]^{4}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\phi[\phi_{0}]^{4}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{5}]^{2}.

Here we make use of the definition

\displaystyle\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{5}]\equiv||\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{5}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\equiv\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

J_{12}
is similar to
J_{3}
in the analysis of pair
(\zeta_{0},\zeta_{1})
and hence one has

\displaystyle J_{12}\lesssim\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\phi[\phi_{0}]^{2}\zeta[\zeta_{5}]^{2}\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]^{2}.

\displaystyle J_{13}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{4}}\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{4}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{4}}\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{4}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{2}]^{2}.

J_{14}
is similar to
J_{4}
in the analysis of pair
(\zeta_{0},\zeta_{1})
and hence one has

\displaystyle J_{14}\lesssim\frac{a^{3}}{|u|^{5}}\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2}\zeta[\zeta_{2}]^{2}\bm{\phi}[\phi_{0}]^{2}+\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{4}}\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2}\zeta[\zeta_{2}]^{2}\underline{\bm{\phi}}[\phi_{1}]^{2}.

J_{15}
is similar to
J_{7}
in the analysis of pair
(\zeta_{0},\zeta_{1})
and hence one has

\displaystyle J_{15}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u|^{4}}\frac{|u|^{2}}{a}\zeta[\zeta_{4}]^{2}a^{2}\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{4}\lesssim\frac{1}{|u|^{3}}\mathcal{O}^{6}.

For terms
\Gamma\zeta_{l}
one need estimate

\displaystyle J_{2}\equiv

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\Gamma(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{l}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\zeta_{m}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\Gamma||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k,i_{l}<k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\zeta_{m}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}=J_{21}+J_{22}+J_{23}.

We have

\displaystyle J_{21}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}(||\Gamma(\lambda,{\underline{\omega}})(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||\tau(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+||\tau(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||\rho(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|}\Gamma[\lambda]^{2}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{1}]^{2}+\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma[\tau]^{2}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{2}]^{2}+\frac{1}{|u|}\Gamma[\tau]^{2}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{4}]^{2}+\frac{1}{a}\Gamma[\rho]^{2}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{5}]^{2}.

\displaystyle J_{22}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}(||\zeta_{1}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},{\underline{\omega}},\lambda)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||\zeta_{2}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\tau||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+||\zeta_{4}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\tau||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||\zeta_{5}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\rho||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{2}}\zeta[\zeta_{1}]^{2}\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}[{\underline{\omega}},{\tilde{\mu}}]+\frac{1}{a}\zeta[\zeta_{1}]^{2}\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}[\lambda]+\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\zeta[\zeta_{2}]^{2}\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}[\tau]+\frac{1}{a}\zeta[\zeta_{4}]^{2}\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}[\tau]+\frac{1}{|u|}\zeta[\zeta_{5}]^{2}\bm{\Gamma}_{11}[\rho].

\displaystyle J_{23}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\mathcal{O}^{2}\mathcal{O}^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{4}.

Collect the results one has

\displaystyle J_{1}\lesssim 1.

For the estimate of
N
one need to analyse

\displaystyle H\equiv\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}Q_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

For terms
\zeta_{1,2,3}\phi_{j}^{2}
, we already have control
which can be bounded by
\frac{\mathcal{O}^{6}}{a}
.
Compared with pair
(\zeta_{0},\zeta_{1})
, one need estimate,

\zeta_{0}\lambda
,
\zeta_{1}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)
,
\zeta_{2}\rho
and
\zeta_{4}\bar{\sigma}
.
One then has

\displaystyle\tilde{H}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\Gamma(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{l}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\zeta_{m}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\Gamma||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k,i_{l}<k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\zeta_{m}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}K\textit{ð}^{i_{2}}\zeta_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}=\tilde{H}_{1}+...+\tilde{H}_{4}.

\displaystyle\tilde{H}_{1}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}(||\lambda(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||\Gamma(\tau,\pi)(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+||\Gamma(\rho,\sigma)(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||\sigma(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}\Gamma[\lambda]^{2}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{0}]^{2}+\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}\Gamma[\tau,\pi]^{2}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{1}]^{2}+\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma[\rho]^{2}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{2}]^{2}+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma[\sigma]^{2}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{2}]^{2}+\frac{a}{|u|}\Gamma[\sigma]^{2}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{4}]^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{4}]^{2}+1.

\displaystyle\tilde{H}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}(||\zeta_{0}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\lambda||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||\zeta_{1}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Gamma(\pi,\tau)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+||\zeta_{2}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Gamma(\rho,\sigma)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||\zeta_{4}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\sigma||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2}\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}[\lambda]+\frac{1}{a^{2}}\zeta[\zeta_{1}]^{2}\bm{\Gamma}_{11}[\tau,\pi]+\frac{1}{a^{2}}\zeta[\zeta_{2}]^{2}\bm{\Gamma}_{11}[\rho,\sigma]+\frac{1}{|u|}\zeta[\zeta_{4}]^{2}\bm{\Gamma}_{11}[\sigma]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{1}]^{2}+\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{1}]+1\lesssim 1.

Here we make use of the control for
\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{1}]
.

\displaystyle\tilde{H}_{3}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}(a\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\Gamma[\lambda]^{2}+\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\zeta[\zeta_{4}]^{2}a\Gamma[\sigma]^{2}+\mathcal{O}^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|}(\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2}\Gamma[\lambda]^{2}+\zeta[\zeta_{4}]^{2}\Gamma[\sigma]^{2})\lesssim 1.

\displaystyle\tilde{H}_{4}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}K||^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-1-i}\zeta_{1}||^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\zeta[\zeta_{1}]^{2}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-1}K||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}\zeta[\zeta_{1}]^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-1}K||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}.

Then we have

\displaystyle H\lesssim\tilde{H}+\frac{\mathcal{O}^{6}}{a}\lesssim\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{4}]^{2}+1.

Collect the results one has

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+(M+N)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+J+H\lesssim\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{4}]^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+1.

Here in the last step we make use of the results in
24
.

∎

Remark 6
.

For terms
\zeta_{l}\phi_{j}^{2}
we have control

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}(\zeta_{l}\phi_{j}^{2})||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{O}^{6}}{a}.

Proposition 24
.

For
0\leq k\leq 11
, one has that

\displaystyle||\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\leq\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{4}}}.

Proof.

For pair
(\zeta_{4},\zeta_{5})
we make use of

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\zeta_{4}

\displaystyle:=-\frac{\zeta_{4}\,{\underline{\omega}}}{2}+\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{5}\,\phi_{0}\,\phi_{1}-\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{2}\,\phi_{1}^{2}-2\,\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{5}\,\phi_{0}\,\bar{\phi}_{1}+2\,\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{4}\,\phi_{1}\,\bar{\phi}_{1}-\zeta_{2}\,\bar{\lambda}-2\,\zeta_{4}\,\mu+\frac{\zeta_{5}\,\tau}{2}+\textit{ð}\,\zeta_{5},

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\,\zeta_{5}

\displaystyle:=\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{5}\,\phi_{0}\,\bar{\phi}_{0}-\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{4}\,\phi_{0}\,\phi_{1}-\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{4}\,\bar{\phi}_{0}\,\phi_{1}+\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{1}\,\phi_{1}^{2}+\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{2}\,\phi_{0}\,\bar{\phi}_{1}-\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{1}\,\phi_{1}\,\bar{\phi}_{1}

\displaystyle\quad-\zeta_{3}\,\lambda-\zeta_{1}\,\mu+2\,\zeta_{4}\,\pi+\zeta_{2}\,\bar{\pi}+\zeta_{5}\,\rho+\frac{\bar{\zeta}_{4}\,\bar{\tau}}{2}+\textit{ð}^{\prime}\,\zeta_{4}.

Applying
\textit{ð}^{k}
and commuting with
þ
and
\textit{þ}^{\prime}
we have that

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{4}+(k+2)\mu\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{1}-\textit{ð}^{k+1}\zeta_{5}=P_{k},

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{5}-\textit{ð}^{\prime}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{4}=Q_{k},

where

\displaystyle P_{k}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\phi_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\zeta_{m}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\zeta_{m},

\displaystyle Q_{k}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{5}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\zeta_{m}+\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\zeta_{m}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}K\textit{ð}^{i_{2}}\zeta_{4}.

Then apply the energy inequality one has

\displaystyle||\frac{1}{|u|}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||\frac{1}{|u|}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\zeta_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||\frac{1}{|u|}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v))}+||\frac{1}{|u|}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\zeta_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\zeta_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}P_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||(a^{\frac{k}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\zeta_{5}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}Q_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\zeta_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}Q_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{0}^{2}+\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{4}]J^{\frac{1}{2}}+\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{5}]H^{\frac{1}{2}},

where

\displaystyle J\equiv\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})},\quad H\equiv\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}Q_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

For terms
\zeta_{l}\phi_{j}^{2}
in
J
and
H
,
make use of the results in Remark
6
we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\textit{ð}^{k}(\zeta_{l}\phi_{j}^{2})||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\textit{ð}^{k}(\zeta_{l}\phi_{j}^{2})||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{O}^{6}}{|u|^{2}}.

For terms
\Gamma\zeta_{l}
we have

\displaystyle I\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||\Gamma(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{l}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||\zeta_{m}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\Gamma||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k,i_{l}<k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\zeta_{m}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}=I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}.

Then one has

\displaystyle I_{1}\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}(

\displaystyle||\mu(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||\Gamma(\lambda,\pi)(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+||\lambda(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{3}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||\Gamma(\tau,\pi,{\underline{\omega}},\lambda)(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+||\Gamma(\tau,\rho,\sigma)(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|}\Gamma[\mu]^{2}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{1}]^{2}+\frac{a}{|u|^{4}}\Gamma[\tau,\pi]^{2}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{2}]^{2}+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma[\lambda]^{2}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{3}]^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}\Gamma[\tau,\pi,{\underline{\omega}}]^{2}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{4}]^{2}+\frac{1}{|u|}\Gamma[\lambda]^{2}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{4}]^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma[\tau,\rho]^{2}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{5}]^{2}+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma[\sigma]^{2}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{5}]^{2}.

\displaystyle I_{2}\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}

\displaystyle(||\zeta_{1}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}{\tilde{\mu}}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||\zeta_{2}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\Gamma(\lambda,\pi)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+||\zeta_{3}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\lambda||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||\zeta_{4}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,{\underline{\omega}},{\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+||\zeta_{5}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\textit{ð}^{k}\Gamma(\tau,\rho,\sigma)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a|u|^{2}}\zeta[\zeta_{1}]^{2}\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}[{\tilde{\mu}}]^{2}+(\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}[\lambda]_{11}^{2}+\frac{1}{|u|^{3}}\bm{\Gamma}[\pi]_{11}^{2})\zeta[\zeta_{2,3}]+\frac{1}{a}\zeta[\zeta_{4,5}]^{2}\bm{\Gamma}_{11}.

\displaystyle I_{3}\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{|u|^{2}}{a}\zeta^{2}\frac{|u|^{2}}{a}\Gamma^{2}\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{O}^{4}}{a}.

And for the last term contain Gaussian curvature we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}K\textit{ð}^{i_{2}}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}K||^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-1-i}\zeta_{4}||^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}\zeta[\zeta_{4}]^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-1}K||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}.

Collect the results above one concludes

\displaystyle J+H\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{O}^{6}}{a},

and that leads to

\displaystyle||\frac{1}{|u|}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||\frac{1}{|u|}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\zeta_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+\frac{\mathcal{O}^{4}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

∎

Proposition 25
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\leq\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{4}}}.

Proof.

For pair
({\tilde{\zeta}}_{4},{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5})
we make use of the following:

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\,\tilde{\zeta}_{4}-\textit{ð}\,\tilde{\zeta}_{5}

\displaystyle=-\,\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{2}\,\phi_{1}^{2}+2\,\mathrm{i}\,\phi_{1}\,\bar{\phi}_{1}\,\tilde{\zeta}_{4}-\mathrm{i}\,\phi_{0}\,\phi_{1}\,\bar{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}-\zeta_{2}\,\bar{\lambda}-\phi_{0}\,\lambda\,\bar{\lambda}-\phi_{1}\,\mu\,\tau

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{{\underline{\omega}}\,\tilde{\zeta}_{4}}{2}-3\,\tilde{\zeta}_{4}\,\mu+\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{5}\,\tau}{2}-2\,\phi_{1}\,\bigl(\textit{ð}\mu\bigr)\,,

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\,\tilde{\zeta}_{5}-\textit{ð}^{\prime}\,\tilde{\zeta}_{4}

\displaystyle=2\Psi_{2}\,\phi_{1}+\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{1}\,\phi_{1}^{2}+\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{2}\,\phi_{0}\,\bar{\phi}_{1}-\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{1}\,\phi_{1}\,\bar{\phi}_{1}-\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\phi}_{0}\,\phi_{1}\,\tilde{\zeta}_{4}-\mathrm{i}\,\phi_{0}\,\phi_{1}\,\bar{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}+\mathrm{i}\,\phi_{0}\,\bar{\phi}_{0}\,\tilde{\zeta}_{5}

\displaystyle\quad-\zeta_{3}\,\lambda+2\,\tilde{\zeta}_{4}\,\pi+\zeta_{2}\,\bar{\pi}+2\,\phi_{1}\,\pi\,\bar{\pi}+\tilde{\zeta}_{5}\,\rho+\phi_{1}\,\mu\,\rho+2\,\phi_{1}\,\lambda\,\sigma+\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{4}\,\bar{\tau}}{2}-\phi_{0}\,\mu\,\bar{\tau}

\displaystyle\quad+2\,\phi_{1}\,\bigl(\textit{ð}\pi\bigr)-\phi_{0}\,\bigl(\textit{ð}^{\prime}\mu\bigr)\,.

Applying
\textit{ð}^{k}
and commuting with
þ
and
\textit{þ}^{\prime}
we have that

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\textit{ð}^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}+(k+3)\mu\textit{ð}^{k}\zeta_{1}-\textit{ð}^{k+1}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}=P_{k},

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\textit{ð}^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}-\textit{ð}^{\prime}\textit{ð}^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}=Q_{k},

where

\displaystyle P_{k}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\phi_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\zeta_{m}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\zeta_{m}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\phi_{1}\textit{ð}^{i_{2}+1}{\tilde{\mu}},

\displaystyle Q_{k}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{5}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\zeta_{m}+\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\zeta_{m}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}+1}\Gamma(\pi,{\tilde{\mu}})+\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{5}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\Gamma

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{1}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{2}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}K\textit{ð}^{i_{2}}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}.

Then apply the energy inequality one has

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\textit{ð}^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\textit{ð}^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+(M+N).

where

\displaystyle M\equiv

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\textit{ð}^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}P_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\textit{ð}^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\bm{\zeta}[{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}]J^{\frac{1}{2}},

and

\displaystyle N\equiv

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\textit{ð}^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}Q_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\textit{ð}^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}Q_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}]H^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Here
0\leq k\leq 10
and we define

\displaystyle J\equiv\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})},\quad H\equiv\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}Q_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

We estimate
J
and
H
together and mainly focus on the top derivative terms.
For terms
\zeta_{m}\phi_{l}^{2}
we have

\displaystyle I_{1}\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{5}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\zeta_{m}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}a^{2}\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{4}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4,5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}a^{2}\mathcal{O}^{6}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{4}}\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{4}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{5}]^{2}+\frac{a^{3}}{|u|^{5}}\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{4}\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{4}]^{2}+\frac{a^{3}}{|u|^{5}}\mathcal{O}^{6}.

For term
\Gamma\zeta_{l}
we have

\displaystyle I_{2}\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\zeta_{m}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\Gamma(\lambda,...)a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\Gamma(\sigma,...)a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k,rest}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda,\sigma,...)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\zeta_{m}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|}\Gamma[\lambda]^{2}\bm{\zeta}[{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}]^{2}+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma[\sigma]^{2}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}]^{2}+\frac{1}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{4}.

For terms
\Gamma^{2}\phi_{j}
, we have

\displaystyle I_{3}\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{5}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\Gamma||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mu\mathcal{D}^{i}\phi_{0,1}\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\Gamma(\tau,\rho)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{3}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\lambda\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\lambda\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\phi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{3}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\lambda\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\phi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{5}=k,rest}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\Gamma||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})},

and we obtain

\displaystyle I_{3}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}(\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{a^{2}}\Gamma[\mu]^{2}\Gamma[\tau,\rho]^{2}a\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2}+\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\Gamma[\lambda]^{2}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\Gamma[\lambda]^{2}a\phi[\phi_{0}]^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\Gamma[\lambda]^{2}a\Gamma[\sigma]^{2}a\phi[\phi_{0}]^{2}+\Gamma[\pi]^{2}\Gamma[\pi]^{2}a\phi[\phi_{1}])

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{6}+\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\mathcal{O}^{6}.

For terms
\phi_{0,1}\textit{ð}{\tilde{\mu}}
and
\phi_{1}\textit{ð}\pi
we have

\displaystyle I_{4}\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}+1}\Gamma||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\pi)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{a}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2}\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}[{\tilde{\mu}}]+\frac{1}{|u|}\phi[\phi_{0,1}]^{2}\bm{\Gamma}_{11}[\pi]+\frac{1}{a^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{4}.

For term
\phi_{1}\Psi_{2}
we have

\displaystyle I_{5}\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{1}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}(a\phi[\phi_{1}]^{2}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+a\mathcal{O}^{4})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\phi[\phi_{1}]^{2}\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[\Psi_{2}]^{2}+\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\mathcal{O}^{4}.

For the term contain the Gaussian curvature we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}a\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\textit{ð}^{i}K\textit{ð}^{k-1-i}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\leq\frac{1}{|u|}\zeta[{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}]^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-1}K||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

Collect the results we obtain

\displaystyle J+H\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{O}^{6}}{a},

and conclude that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\textit{ð}^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\textit{ð}^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+\frac{\mathcal{O}^{4}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

∎

6.3
Energy estimate for curvature

Proposition 26
.

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}^{2}+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+1.

Proof.

For the pair
(\Psi_{L},\Psi_{R})
where
\Psi_{L}\in\{\Psi_{0},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1},\Psi_{2},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}\}

and
\Psi_{R}\in\{{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1},\Psi_{2},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3},\Psi_{4}\}
we make use of

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\Psi_{L}-\textit{ð}\Psi_{R}=

\displaystyle\phi_{j}\textit{ð}\zeta_{l}+\Gamma\Psi_{l}+\Psi_{l}\phi_{j}^{2}+\zeta_{j}^{2}+\zeta_{j}\phi_{l}^{3}+\zeta_{j}\phi_{l}\Gamma+\phi_{j}^{2}\Gamma^{2},

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\Psi_{R}-\textit{ð}^{\prime}\Psi_{L}=

\displaystyle\phi_{j}\textit{ð}\zeta_{l}+\Gamma\Psi_{l}+\Psi_{l}\phi_{j}^{2}+\zeta_{j}^{2}+\zeta_{j}\phi_{l}^{3}+\zeta_{j}\phi_{l}\Gamma+\phi_{j}^{2}\Gamma^{2}.

Applying
\textit{ð}^{k}
and commuting with
þ
and
\textit{þ}^{\prime}
we have that

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}\textit{ð}^{k}\Psi_{L}+\left((2s_{2}(\Psi_{L})+k+1\right)\mu\textit{ð}^{k}\Psi_{R}-\textit{ð}^{k+1}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}=P_{k},

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\textit{ð}^{k}\Psi_{R}-\textit{ð}^{\prime}\textit{ð}^{k}\Psi_{L}=Q_{k},

where
P_{k}
and
Q_{k}
have the following form

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}+1}\zeta_{l}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{l}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{5}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{j_{1}}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{j_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\Psi_{l}+\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\zeta_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\zeta_{j}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+..+i_{6}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{j_{1}}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{j_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\phi_{j_{3}}\textit{ð}^{i_{6}}\zeta_{l}+\sum_{i_{1}+..+i_{5}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\zeta_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\Gamma

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+..+i_{6}=k}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{j_{1}}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{j_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\Gamma\textit{ð}^{i_{6}}\Gamma+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}K\textit{ð}^{i_{2}}\Psi_{L}.

The make use of the energy inequality one has

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\Psi_{L}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\Psi_{R}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\Psi_{L}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\Psi_{R}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u))}+M+N,

where

\displaystyle M\equiv

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}P_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})},

and

\displaystyle N\equiv

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}Q_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

For pair
(\Psi_{0},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1})
, one need multiply extra
\frac{1}{a}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v))}+\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u))}+\frac{1}{a}(M+N),

and we have

\displaystyle M\leq

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}=\frac{a}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]J^{\frac{1}{2}},

and

\displaystyle N\leq

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}Q_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}=a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]H^{\frac{1}{2}}.

For the rest pairs, i.e.
(\Psi^{\prime}_{L},\Psi^{\prime}_{R})
which denote pair
({\tilde{\Psi}}_{1},\Psi_{2})
,
(\Psi_{2},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3})

and
({\tilde{\Psi}}_{3},\Psi_{4})
one has

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\Psi^{\prime}_{L}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\Psi^{\prime}_{R}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\Psi^{\prime}_{L}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u^{\prime}}(0,v))}+\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\textit{ð})^{k}\Psi^{\prime}_{R}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{v^{\prime}}(u_{\infty},u))}+J+H+\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}.

We estimate
J
and
H
together.

For terms
\Gamma\Psi
,
because the general properties of connections and curvatures are the same
to that shown in, hence one can make use of the results in the paper directly and obtain the control by

\frac{\mathcal{O}^{4}}{a}+1
.

For term
K\Psi_{L}
one has

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}a\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\textit{ð}^{i}K\textit{ð}^{k-1-i}\Psi_{L}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\Psi[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1},\Psi_{2},\Psi_{3}]^{2}+\frac{a^{3}}{|u|^{3}}\Psi[\Psi_{0}]^{2})||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-1}K||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}.

For terms
\phi_{j}\textit{ð}\zeta_{l}
, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}+1}\zeta_{l}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\zeta[\zeta_{4,5}]^{2}\lesssim 1+\frac{1}{a}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\zeta[\zeta_{4,5}]^{2}.

Details can be found in
B.1
.

For terms
\phi_{j}^{2}\Psi_{l}
one has

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{5}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{j_{1}}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{j_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\Psi_{l}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{4}a\Psi[\Psi_{0}]^{2}\leq\frac{a^{4}}{|u|^{5}}\mathcal{O}^{6}.

Details can be found in
B.2
.

Next, appendix
B.3
shows that
terms
\zeta_{l}^{2}
can be bounded by
\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\zeta[\zeta_{j}]^{4}
.
Terms
\zeta_{j}\phi_{l}^{3}
can be bounded by
1/a
which are shown in
B.4
.

For terms
\zeta_{j}\phi_{l}\Gamma
, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{5}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\zeta_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\Gamma||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\leq\frac{a}{|u|}\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\Gamma[\mu]^{2}+\frac{1}{a}\mathcal{O}^{6}.

See
B.5
.

For terms
\phi_{j}^{2}\mu\Gamma
, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{6}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{l_{1}}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{l_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\mu\textit{ð}^{i_{6}}\Gamma||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{8}.

See
B.6
.

Now collect the results above one can conclude that

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}^{2}+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+1.

With the results above one can estimate the top derivative of Gaussian curvature
K=\mathcal{K}+\bar{\mathcal{K}}

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}=\mathrm{i}\bar{\zeta}_{1}\phi_{1}-\mathrm{i}\zeta_{1}\bar{\phi}_{1}+\mathrm{i}\bar{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}\phi_{0}-\mathrm{i}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}\bar{\phi}_{0}-\Psi_{2}+\mu\rho-\lambda\sigma.

We have

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\mathcal{K}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{k}||a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\zeta_{1}\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\phi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}||a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\phi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}||a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\mu\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}||a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\lambda\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|}\zeta[\zeta_{1}]a^{\frac{1}{2}}\phi[\phi_{1}]+\frac{1}{|u|}\zeta[{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}]a^{\frac{1}{2}}\phi[\phi_{0}]+\frac{1}{|u|}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\phi[\phi_{1}]||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{|u|}\frac{|u|^{2}}{a}\Gamma[\mu]\frac{a}{|u|}\Gamma[\rho]+\frac{1}{|u|}\frac{|u|}{a}\Gamma[{\tilde{\mu}}]\Gamma[\rho]+\frac{1}{|u|}\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Gamma[\lambda]a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma[\sigma]

\displaystyle+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+1.

Then, integrating along the light cone one has

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\mathcal{K}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}\lesssim

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+1\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\mathcal{K}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}+1\lesssim 1.

Hence one obtains the control of the Gaussian curvature.

∎

Then we obtain the main existence result:

Theorem 3
.

(Existence result)

Given
\mathcal{I}
, there exists a sufficiently large

a_{0}=a_{0}(\mathcal{I})
, such that for
0\leq a_{0}\leq a
and initial
data

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{0}\equiv\sum_{j=0}^{1}\sum_{i=0}^{15}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||\textit{þ}^{j}(|u_{\infty}|\mathcal{D})^{i}(\sigma,\phi_{0})||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}\leq\mathcal{I},

along the outgoing initial null hypersurface
u=u_{\infty}
and
Minkowskian initial data along the ingoing initial null hypersurface
v=0
,
then the Einstein-Weyl spinor system has a solution in the causal diamond

\displaystyle\mathbb{D}=\big\{(u,v)|u_{\infty}\leq u\leq-a/4,\ \ 0\leq v\leq 1\big\}.

7
Trapped surface formation

In this section, we prove the following

Theorem 4

(
Trapped surface formation
)
.

Given
\mathcal{I}
, there exists a sufficiently large

a=a(\mathcal{I})
such that if
0\leq a_{0}\leq a
, and the initial
data satisfies

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{0}\equiv\sum_{j=0}^{1}\sum_{i=0}^{15}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||\textit{þ}^{j}(|u_{\infty}|\mathcal{D})^{i}(\sigma,\phi_{0})||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}\leq\mathcal{I}

along the outgoing initial null hypersurface
u=u_{\infty}
and is
Minkowskian initial data along ingoing initial null hypersurface
v=0
,
with

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\left(|u_{\infty}|^{2}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+2\mathrm{i}\phi_{0}\textit{þ}\bar{\phi}_{0}-2\mathrm{i}\bar{\phi}_{0}\textit{þ}\phi_{0})|\right)(u_{\infty},v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\geq a,

uniformly for any point on the outgoing initial null hypersurface

u=u_{\infty}
, then
we have that
\mathcal{S}_{-a/4,1}
is a trapped surface.

Proof.

We start with the frame coefficient.
Since the properties are the same as in the Einstein–Scalar case,
we state the results directly and refer to
[
8
,
15
]
.

\displaystyle|Q|-1\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|},\quad|P^{\mathcal{A}}(u,v)|\lesssim|P^{\mathcal{A}}(u,0)|+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathcal{O},\quad|C^{\mathcal{A}}|\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\mathcal{O}.

for a point
(x^{2},x^{3})\in\mathcal{S}
in a coordinate area.
From the equation (
43h
),

\displaystyle\textit{þ}\,\rho

\displaystyle=2\,\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{0}\,\phi_{0}-2\,\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{0}\,\bar{\phi}_{0}+\rho^{2}+\sigma\,\bar{\sigma},

one defines

\displaystyle F\equiv|u|^{2}Q^{-1}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+2\mathrm{i}\bar{\zeta}_{0}\phi_{0}-2\mathrm{i}\zeta_{0}\bar{\phi}_{0}).

Then from
{\bm{n}}=\partial_{u}+C^{\mathcal{A}}\partial_{\mathcal{A}}
, one has

\displaystyle\partial_{u}F=\textit{þ}^{\prime}F-C^{\mathcal{A}}\partial_{\mathcal{A}}F\equiv I_{1}+I_{2}.

From equation
\textit{þ}^{\prime}\sigma
,
\textit{þ}^{\prime}\phi_{0}
,
\textit{þ}^{\prime}\zeta_{0}
as well as
\textit{þ}^{\prime}Q
, one can
compute that

\displaystyle\textit{þ}^{\prime}F=|u|^{2}Q^{-1}M

where the expression of M is given in terms of
A.6
.
Using the existence theorem
3
we calculate and obtain

\displaystyle|M|\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{4}},

and hence

\displaystyle|I_{1}|\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}.

For the analysis of
I_{2}
, because the property of
\phi_{0}
and
\zeta_{0}
is the same with that of
\varphi_{0}
,
hence we obtain

\displaystyle|I_{2}|\leq\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}

which leads to

|\partial_{u}F|\leq\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\ll\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{|u|^{2}}.

Then assume that the initial data satisfy

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\left(|u_{\infty}|^{2}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+2\mathrm{i}\bar{\zeta}_{0}\phi_{0}-2\mathrm{i}\zeta_{0}\bar{\phi}_{0})|\right)(u_{\infty},v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\geq a.

Using the above analysis of
F
, for sufficiently large a, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\left(|u|^{2}Q^{-1}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+2\mathrm{i}\bar{\zeta}_{0}\phi_{0}-2\mathrm{i}\zeta_{0}\bar{\phi}_{0})|\right)(u,v^{\prime},x^{2},x^{3})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\left(|u_{\infty}|^{2}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+2\mathrm{i}\bar{\zeta}_{0}\phi_{0}-2\mathrm{i}\zeta_{0}\bar{\phi}_{0})|\right)(u_{\infty},v^{\prime},x^{2},x^{3})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}-\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{|u|}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle a-\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{|u|}\geq a-\frac{4a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{a}\geq\frac{a}{2}.

When
u=-\frac{a}{4}
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\left(Q^{-1}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+2\mathrm{i}\bar{\zeta}_{0}\phi_{0}-2\mathrm{i}\zeta_{0}\bar{\phi}_{0})\right)|_{(-\frac{a}{4},v^{\prime},x^{2},x^{3})}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\geq\frac{8}{a}.

With the results above, now we move to the calculation of expansion.
For the outgoing expansion defined as

\bm{\theta}_{l}\equiv\bm{/\kern-6.66998pt\sigma}^{ab}\nabla_{a}l_{b}=-\rho-\bar{\rho}=-2\rho
,
initially on
\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}
,
one makes use of
Q=1
, and the initial data for
\phi_{0}
,
\zeta_{0}

and
\rho(u,0,x^{2},x^{3})=-\frac{1}{|u|}
and has

\displaystyle\rho(u_{\infty},v,x^{2},x^{3})

\displaystyle=\rho(u_{\infty},0,x^{2},x^{3})+\int_{0}^{1}\frac{\partial\rho}{\partial v}\mathrm{d}v=-\frac{1}{|u_{\infty}|}+\int_{0}^{v}\textit{þ}\rho\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{|u_{\infty}|}+\int_{0}^{v}\left(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+2\,\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{0}\,\phi_{0}-2\,\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{0}\,\bar{\phi}_{0}\right)|_{(-\frac{a}{4},v^{\prime},x^{2},x^{3})}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim-\frac{1}{|u_{\infty}|}+\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|^{2}}<0,

which means the outgoing expansion is positive along
\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}
.
Then on
\mathcal{N}_{-a/4}
we have that

\displaystyle\rho(-\frac{a}{4},1,x^{2},x^{3})=

\displaystyle\rho(-\frac{a}{4},0,x^{2},x^{3})+\int_{0}^{1}\frac{\partial\rho}{\partial v}\mathrm{d}v=-\frac{4}{a}+\int_{0}^{1}Q^{-1}\textit{þ}\rho\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-\frac{4}{a}+\int_{0}^{1}Q^{-1}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+2\,\mathrm{i}\,\bar{\zeta}_{0}\,\phi_{0}-2\,\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{0}\,\bar{\phi}_{0})|_{(-\frac{a}{4},v^{\prime},x^{2},x^{3})}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-\frac{4}{a}+\frac{8}{a}=\frac{4}{a}>0.

That leads to
\bm{\theta}_{l}=-2\rho<0
, i.e. for any
(x^{2},x^{3})
,
the outgoing expansion on
\mathcal{N}_{-a/4}
is negative.
For the ingoing expansion
\theta_{{\bm{n}}}
which is defined by

\theta_{{\bm{n}}}\equiv\bm{/\kern-6.66998pt\sigma}^{ab}\nabla_{a}n_{b}=\mu+\bar{\mu}=2\mu
,
we have the estimate

\displaystyle||{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}=||\mu+\frac{1}{|u|}||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\frac{1}{|u|^{2}},

which leads to

\displaystyle\mu(-\frac{a}{4},v,x^{2},x^{3})<0,\quad\mu(u_{\infty},v,x^{2},x^{3})<0

for any
(x^{2},x^{3})
. This means that the ingoing expansion is always negative.
Then we conclude that the 2-sphere
\mathcal{S}_{-\frac{a}{4},1}
is a trapped surface.
In summary, we have derived the dynamical process that governs the emergence of trapped surfaces
in the Einstein–Weyl model, thereby providing a description of their formation from absence to existence.
∎

Remark 7

(Low-angular momentum argument)
.

Make use of the existence result one has that

\displaystyle|\phi_{0}|\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|},\quad|\phi_{1}|\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{2}},\quad|\zeta_{0}|\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|},\quad|\zeta_{1},\zeta_{3}|\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}},

then one can calculate that

\displaystyle\Phi_{00}=2\mathrm{i}\bigl(\bar{\zeta}_{0}\phi_{0}-\zeta_{0}\bar{\phi}_{0}\bigr)\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{2}},\quad\Phi_{01}=\mathrm{i}\bigl(2\bar{\zeta}_{1}\phi_{0}-\zeta_{3}\bar{\phi}_{0}-\zeta_{0}\bar{\phi}_{1}\bigr)\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{3}},

hence

\displaystyle|\Phi_{01}|\sim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}|\Phi_{00}|

where
a
is a universal large number.
Together with the initial data assumption, we have shown that such relation keeps and
physically, this means that compared with the energy density,
angular momentum density is small physically.
