\displaystyle\quad-2\,\mathrm{i}\,\overline{\phi}_{0}\,\tilde{\zeta}_{4}\,\rho+2\,\mathrm{i}\,\phi_{0}\,\overline{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}\,\rho-2\,\mathrm{i}\,\overline{\zeta}_{3}\,\phi_{1}\,\sigma-\lambda\,\rho\,\sigma+2\,\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{3}\,\overline{\phi}_{1}\,\overline{\sigma}-\overline{\lambda}\,\rho\,\overline{\sigma}+2\,{\underline{\omega}}\,\sigma\,\overline{\sigma}

\displaystyle\quad-2\,{\tilde{\mu}}\,\sigma\,\overline{\sigma}-2\,\mathrm{i}\,\overline{\zeta}_{3}\,\phi_{0}\,\tau+5\,\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{1}\,\overline{\phi}_{0}\,\tau-2\,\mathrm{i}\,\overline{\zeta}_{0}\,\phi_{1}\,\tau-\overline{\sigma}\,\tau^{2}-5\,\mathrm{i}\,\overline{\zeta}_{1}\,\phi_{0}\,\bar{\tau}+2\,\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{3}\,\overline{\phi}_{0}\,\tau

\displaystyle\quad+2\,\mathrm{i}\,\zeta_{0}\,\overline{\phi}_{1}\,\bar{\tau}-\sigma\bar{\tau}^{2}-2\,\mathrm{i}\,\overline{\phi}_{0}\,(\textit{ð}\,\zeta_{1})+\overline{\sigma}\,(\textit{ð}\,\overline{\tau})+2\,\mathrm{i}\,\phi_{0}\,(\textit{ð}^{\prime}\,\overline{\zeta}_{1})+\sigma\,(\textit{ð}^{\prime}\,\overline{\tau}).

Appendix B
Energy estimate support for Bianchi identity

In this section we show details of analysis of matter fields in Prop.
26
.

B.1
Term
\phi_{j}\textit{ð}\zeta_{l}

For the top derivative terms we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{5}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{11}\zeta_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\leq

\displaystyle\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\zeta_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{0}]^{2}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{3}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\leq\frac{a^{3}}{|u|^{3}}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{0}]^{2}

Here

\displaystyle{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{0}]^{2}\equiv\frac{1}{a}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{5}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{11}\zeta_{1,3}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\leq

\displaystyle\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\zeta_{1,3}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{2}}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\frac{1}{a}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\zeta_{1,3}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{2}}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{1,3}]^{2}

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{5}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{11}\zeta_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\leq

\displaystyle\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\zeta_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\zeta_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{2}]^{2}

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{5}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{11}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\leq

\displaystyle\phi[\phi_{i}]^{2}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{11}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{11}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{4}]^{2},

Here

\displaystyle\bm{\zeta}[\zeta_{4}]^{2}\equiv\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}.

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{5}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{11}\zeta_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\leq

\displaystyle\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\zeta_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{\frac{11}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{11}\zeta_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\underline{{\bm{\zeta}}}[\zeta_{5}]^{2},

Here

\displaystyle\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[\zeta_{5}]^{2}\equiv\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\zeta_{5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

For the non-top-derivative terms

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k,i_{4}<k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}+1}\zeta_{l}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\zeta[\zeta_{4,5}]^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{a}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\zeta[\zeta_{4,5}]^{2}.

B.2
Term
\phi_{j}^{2}\Psi_{l}

The top derivative of curvature

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\phi_{j}^{2}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{l}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}a\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}a\phi[\phi_{l}]^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{l}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{4}}{|u^{\prime}|^{6}}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{4}\frac{1}{a}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{4}}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{4}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{1,2,3,4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{a^{4}}{|u|^{5}}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{4}\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]^{2}+\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{4}}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{4}\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[\Psi_{1,2,3,4}]^{2}

For the non-top-derivative terms

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{5}=k,i_{5}<k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{j_{1}}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{j_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\Psi_{l}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{4}a\Psi[\Psi_{0}]^{2}\leq\frac{a^{4}}{|u|^{5}}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{4}\Psi[\Psi_{0}]^{2}.

B.3
Term
\zeta_{l}^{2}

The large terms are
\zeta_{0}\zeta_{l}
where
l\neq 0
. We have

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\zeta_{j_{1}}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\zeta_{j_{2}}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2}\zeta[\zeta_{j}]^{2}\leq\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\zeta[\zeta_{j}]^{4}.

B.4
Term
\zeta_{j}\phi_{l}^{3}

The large terms are
\zeta_{0}\phi_{l}^{3}
. We have

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{6}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\zeta_{j}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{l_{1}}\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\phi_{l_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{6}}\phi_{l_{3}}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{6}}a^{4}\mathcal{O}^{8}\leq\frac{a^{5}}{|u|^{7}}\mathcal{O}^{8}.

B.5
Term
\zeta_{j}\phi_{l}\Gamma

The large terms are
\zeta_{0}\phi_{1}\mu
in eq
\textit{þ}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}
,

\zeta_{0}\phi_{0}\mu
in eq
\textit{þ}\Psi_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{5}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\zeta_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\Gamma||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}a\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2}a\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{a^{2}}\Gamma[\mu]^{2}\leq\frac{a}{|u|}\zeta[\zeta_{0}]^{2}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\Gamma[\mu]^{2}.

Moreover, note here
\zeta_{j}
does contain
{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4,5}
.
For terms contain
{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4,5}
, the large terms are
{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4,5}\phi_{j}\mu
in

\textit{þ}^{\prime}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}
and
\textit{þ}^{\prime}\Psi_{2}
.
We have control

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{5}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4,5}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{l}\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\Gamma||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}a\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{a^{2}}\Gamma[\mu]^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4,5}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}a\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{a^{2}}\Gamma[\mu]^{2}\zeta[{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4,5}]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\Gamma[\mu]^{2}\bm{\zeta}[{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4}]^{2}+\frac{1}{a}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\Gamma[\mu]^{2}\underline{\bm{\zeta}}[{\tilde{\zeta}}_{5}]^{2}+\frac{1}{a}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{2}\Gamma[\mu]^{2}\zeta[{\tilde{\zeta}}_{4,5}]^{2}.

B.6
Term
\phi_{j}^{2}\mu\Gamma(\lambda,\sigma,\rho,\tau)

The large terms are
\phi_{j}^{2}\mu\lambda
and we have control

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{6}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\textit{ð}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{3}}\phi_{l_{1}}\textit{ð}^{i_{4}}\phi_{l_{2}}\textit{ð}^{i_{5}}\mu\textit{ð}^{i_{6}}\Gamma||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{6}}a^{2}\phi[\phi_{j}]^{4}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{a^{2}}\Gamma[\mu]^{2}\frac{|u|^{2}}{a}\Gamma[\lambda]^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{8}.
